theorem CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (unionmap : List (Int × identifier)) (funptrmap : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int)
  (ty : ctype) (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel lemFuel unionmap funptrmap addr ty bytes =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 =>
      fuelExhaustedWith "CerbMem.reconstructValue: fuel exhausted"
        (CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty)
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match ty with
      | Ctype a (Basic (Integer ity)) =>
        have signed := CerberusImpl.is_signed_ity ity;
        match CerbMem.bytesToInt bytes signed with
        | some n =>
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ity
            (CerbMem.IntegerValue.IV (CerbMem.provFromIntegerBytes bytes) n)
        | none => CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty
      | Ctype a (Basic (Floating fty)) =>
        match CerbMem.bytesToInt bytes false with
        | some n =>
          have bits := n.toNat.toUInt64;
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVfloating fty (Float.ofBits bits)
        | none => CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty
      | Ctype a (Pointer a_1 pointeeCty) =>
        match CerbMem.bytesToInt bytes false with
        | some 0 =>
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer pointeeCty
            (CerbMem.PointerValue.PV CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_none
              (CerbMem.PointerValueBase.PVnull pointeeCty))
        | some ptrAddr =>
          match CerbMem.splitBytesProv bytes with
          | (prov, _validPtrProv) =>
            match pointeeCty with
            | Ctype a (Function a_2 a_3 a_4) =>
              match
                List.find?
                  (fun x =>
                    match x with
                    | (a, snd) => a == ptrAddr)
                  funptrmap with
              | some (fst, fileDig, name) =>
                CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer pointeeCty
                  (CerbMem.PointerValue.PV prov
                    (CerbMem.PointerValueBase.PVfunction
                      (Symbol fileDig ptrAddr.toNat (SD_Id name))))
              | none =>
                panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbMem" "CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel" 705 18
                  (toString "unknown function pointer: " ++ toString ptrAddr)
            | x =>
              CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer pointeeCty
                (CerbMem.PointerValue.PV prov
                  (CerbMem.PointerValueBase.PVconcrete none ↑ptrAddr.toNat))
        | none => CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty
      | Ctype a (Array0 elemCty (some n)) =>
        have nNat := n.toNat;
        have elemSize := CerbMem.sizeofCtype elemCty;
        if (elemSize == 0) = true then CerbMem.MemValue.MVarray []
        else
          have elems :=
            List.map
              (fun i =>
                have start := i * elemSize;
                have elemBytes := List.take elemSize (List.drop start bytes);
                CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel lemFuel unionmap funptrmap addr elemCty elemBytes)
              (List.range nNat);
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVarray elems
      | Ctype a (Atomic innerCty) =>
        CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel lemFuel unionmap funptrmap addr innerCty bytes
      | Ctype a Byte =>
        match CerbMem.bytesToInt (List.take 1 bytes) false with
        | some n =>
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger Char0
            (CerbMem.IntegerValue.IV (CerbMem.provFromIntegerBytes (List.take 1 bytes)) n)
        | none => CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty
      | Ctype a (Struct tagSym) =>
        match CerbMem.offsetsof (fmapElements (CerbTags.tagDefs ())) tagSym true with
        | (offs, snd) =>
          match
            List.foldl
              (fun acc memb =>
                match acc with
                | (revXs, prevEnd) =>
                  match memb with
                  | (ident, membTy, off) =>
                    have pad := off - prevEnd;
                    have membBytes := List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype membTy) (List.drop off bytes);
                    have mval :=
                      CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel lemFuel unionmap funptrmap (addr + ↑pad)
                        membTy membBytes;
                    ((ident, membTy, mval) :: revXs, off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype membTy))
              ([], 0) offs with
          | (revXs, snd) => CerbMem.MemValue.MVstruct tagSym revXs.reverse
      | Ctype a (Union0 tagSym) =>
        match
          List.find?
            (fun x =>
              match x with
              | (s, snd) => symbolEquality s tagSym)
            (fmapElements (CerbTags.tagDefs ())) with
        | some (fst, fst_1, UnionDef membrs) =>
          match membrs with
          | [] =>
            panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbMem" "CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel" 756 14
              "CerbMem.reconstructValue: empty UnionDef (OCaml: match failure)"
          | (firstIdent, fst, fst_2, fst_3, firstTy) :: tail =>
            match
              match
                List.find?
                  (fun x =>
                    match x with
                    | (a, snd) => a == addr)
                  unionmap with
              | none => (firstIdent, firstTy)
              | some (fst, membr) =>
                match
                  List.find?
                    (fun x =>
                      match x with
                      | (i, snd) => idEqual i membr)
                    membrs with
                | some (i, fst, fst_4, fst_5, t) => (i, t)
                | none =>
                  panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbMem" "CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel" 766 22
                    "CerbMem.reconstructValue: recorded union member not in UnionDef (OCaml: assert false)" with
            | (membIdent, membTy) =>
              have mval :=
                CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel lemFuel unionmap funptrmap addr membTy
                  (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype membTy) bytes);
              CerbMem.MemValue.MVunion tagSym membIdent mval
        | x =>
          panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbMem" "CerbMem.reconstructValue_lemFuel" 770 11
            "CerbMem.reconstructValue: Union tag not a UnionDef (OCaml: assert false)"
      | x => CerbMem.MemValue.MVunspecified ty
----
theorem CerbND.runNDFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a info err cs st : Type} (fuel : Nat) (m : ndM a info err cs st) (st0 : st),
  CerbND.runNDFuel fuel m st0 =
    match fuel with
    | 0 =>
      panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbND" "CerbND.runNDFuel" 95 4
        "CerbND.runNDFuel: ND-tree depth budget exhausted — this is FUEL EXHAUSTION, not an empty behavior set (see CerbND.lean header; budget: ndDefaultFuel)"
    | fuel.succ =>
      match m with
      | ND m_act =>
        match m_act st0 with
        | (NDactive result, st') => [(Active result, [], st')]
        | (NDkilled reason, st') => [(Killed st' reason, [], st')]
        | (NDnd _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
        | (NDguard _info _constraint continuation, st') => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel continuation st'
        | (NDbranch _info _constraint left right, st') =>
          CerbND.runNDFuel fuel left st' ++ CerbND.runNDFuel fuel right st'
        | (NDstep _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.mk.inj :
∀ {lastAddr nextId lastAddr_1 nextId_1 : Int},
  { lastAddr := lastAddr, nextId := nextId } = { lastAddr := lastAddr_1, nextId := nextId_1 } →
    lastAddr = lastAddr_1 ∧ nextId = nextId_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.mk.injEq :
∀ (lastAddr nextId lastAddr_1 nextId_1 : Int),
  ({ lastAddr := lastAddr, nextId := nextId } = { lastAddr := lastAddr_1, nextId := nextId_1 }) =
    (lastAddr = lastAddr_1 ∧ nextId = nextId_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {lastAddr nextId lastAddr' nextId' : Int} →
    { lastAddr := lastAddr, nextId := nextId } = { lastAddr := lastAddr', nextId := nextId' } →
      (lastAddr = lastAddr' → nextId = nextId' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (lastAddr nextId : Int),
  sizeOf { lastAddr := lastAddr, nextId := nextId } = 1 + sizeOf lastAddr + sizeOf nextId
----

CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor → Int
----
def CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor} →
    t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor.noConfusionType :
Sort u → CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor → CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor → Sort u
----

    (t : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor) → motive t

inductive CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Int → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.alloc :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∃ al,
      σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
        al.base = c.addr ∧
          al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
            al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.bytes :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = c.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                  (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                    (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                      (dec_indep :
                          ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                        motive ⋯) →
            motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dead :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dec_indep :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes = CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.len :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false →
    (∃ al,
        σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
          al.base = c.addr ∧
            al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
              al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
      CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false →
        c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
          CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes →
            (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                  CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.nonAtomic :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.ofParts :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c) →
    c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
      CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes →
        (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes = CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
          CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----

            (alloc :
                ∃ al,
                  σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                    al.base = c.addr ∧

              (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →

                    (dec_indep :
                        ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                            CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                      motive ⋯) →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) → motive t

CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop

def CerberusHeapLang.Coh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) →
        ((cells :
              ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
            (disj :
                ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  id1 ≠ id2 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                      Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
              motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.cells :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.disj :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      id1 ≠ id2 →
        Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2
----

∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},

      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
    (∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        id1 ≠ id2 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
            Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.store :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (i : Int)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
      CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
          (Iris.Std.insert m i
            { addr := c.addr, ty := c.ty, bytes := (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.store_interior :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (i : Int)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (off : Nat) (img : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
      off + img.length ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
        (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty
                (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img c.bytes) =
              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell
                { addr := c.addr, ty := c.ty,
                  bytes := CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img c.bytes }) →
          CerberusHeapLang.Coh (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ (c.addr + ↑off) img)
            (Iris.Std.insert m i
              { addr := c.addr, ty := c.ty, bytes := CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img c.bytes })
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.CohG :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem →
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell →
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.bytes :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte), Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ k = b
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CohG.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
    {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte} →
      {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor} →
        {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk → Sort u} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk) →
            ((metas :
                  ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                    Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) →
                (metas_disj :
                    ∀ (i j : Int) (mci mcj : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                      i ≠ j →
                        Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mci →
                          Iris.Std.get? mm j = some mcj → CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint mci mcj) →
                  (bytes :
                      ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                        Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ k = b) →
                    (cursor_key :
                        ∀ (k : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                          Iris.Std.get? mk k = some c → k = 0) →
                      (cursor :
                          ∀ (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                            Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c →
                              c.lastAddr = σ.lastAddress ∧ c.nextId = σ.nextAllocId) →
                        (cur_dead :
                            Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                              ∀ (id : Int),
                                σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
                          (cur_alloc :
                              Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.allocations.get? id = none) →
                            (cur_meta_lt :
                                Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                  ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                    Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → id < σ.nextAllocId) →
                              (cur_byte_lo :
                                  Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                                      Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → σ.lastAddress ≤ k) →
                                (cur_meta_lo :
                                    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                      ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                        Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → σ.lastAddress ≤ mc.addr) →
                                  motive ⋯) →
              motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.create :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (pref : prefix0) (alignN : Int) (ty : ctype),
      0 < CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty →
        CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy ty = false →
          Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some { lastAddr := σ.lastAddress, nextId := σ.nextAllocId } →
            CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) ≠ 0 →
              CerberusHeapLang.CohG
                (CerbMem.writeBytesTo
                  { nextAllocId := σ.nextAllocId + 1, nextIota := σ.nextIota,
                    lastAddress :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty),
                    allocations :=
                      σ.allocations.insert σ.nextAllocId
                        {
                          base :=
                            CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN
                              (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty),
                          size := ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty), ty := some ty, prefix_ := pref },
                    iotaMap := σ.iotaMap, funptrmap := σ.funptrmap, varargs := σ.varargs,
                    nextVarargsId := σ.nextVarargsId, bytemap := σ.bytemap,
                    lastUsedUnionMembers := σ.lastUsedUnionMembers,
                    deadAllocations := σ.deadAllocations, dynamicAddrs := σ.dynamicAddrs,
                    lastUsed := σ.lastUsed, requested := σ.requested }
                  (CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty))
                  (List.replicate (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) CerberusHeapLang.undefByte))
                (Iris.Std.insert mm σ.nextAllocId
                  {
                    addr :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty),
                    ty := ty })
                (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union
                  (CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap
                    (CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty))
                    (List.replicate (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) CerberusHeapLang.undefByte))
                  mb)
                (Iris.Std.insert mk 0
                  {
                    lastAddr :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.freshBase σ.lastAddress alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty),
                    nextId := σ.nextAllocId + 1 })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cur_alloc :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none → ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.allocations.get? id = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cur_byte_lo :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
      ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte), Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → σ.lastAddress ≤ k
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cur_dead :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
      ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cur_meta_lo :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
      ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
        Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → σ.lastAddress ≤ mc.addr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cur_meta_lt :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
      ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
        Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → id < σ.nextAllocId
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cursor :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
      Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c → c.lastAddr = σ.lastAddress ∧ c.nextId = σ.nextAllocId
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.cursor_key :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (k : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor), Iris.Std.get? mk k = some c → k = 0
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.metas :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
      Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.metas_disj :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (i j : Int) (mci mcj : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
      i ≠ j →
        Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mci →
          Iris.Std.get? mm j = some mcj → CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint mci mcj
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CohG.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  (∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
      Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) →
    (∀ (i j : Int) (mci mcj : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
        i ≠ j →
          Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mci →
            Iris.Std.get? mm j = some mcj → CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint mci mcj) →
      (∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
          Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ k = b) →
        (∀ (k : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor), Iris.Std.get? mk k = some c → k = 0) →
          (∀ (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
              Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c → c.lastAddr = σ.lastAddress ∧ c.nextId = σ.nextAllocId) →
            (Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                  ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.allocations.get? id = none) →
                (Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                    ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                      Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → id < σ.nextAllocId) →
                  (Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                      ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                        Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → σ.lastAddress ≤ k) →
                    (Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                        ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                          Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → σ.lastAddress ≤ mc.addr) →
                      CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.CohG.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
    {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte} →
      {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor} →
        {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk → Sort u} →
          ((metas :
                ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                  Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) →
              (metas_disj :
                  ∀ (i j : Int) (mci mcj : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                    i ≠ j →
                      Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mci →
                        Iris.Std.get? mm j = some mcj → CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint mci mcj) →
                (bytes :
                    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                      Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ k = b) →
                  (cursor_key :
                      ∀ (k : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                        Iris.Std.get? mk k = some c → k = 0) →
                    (cursor :
                        ∀ (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                          Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c →
                            c.lastAddr = σ.lastAddress ∧ c.nextId = σ.nextAllocId) →
                      (cur_dead :
                          Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                            ∀ (id : Int),
                              σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
                        (cur_alloc :
                            Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                              ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.allocations.get? id = none) →
                          (cur_meta_lt :
                              Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                  Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → id < σ.nextAllocId) →
                            (cur_byte_lo :
                                Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                  ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                                    Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → σ.lastAddress ≤ k) →
                              (cur_meta_lo :
                                  Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                    ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                      Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → σ.lastAddress ≤ mc.addr) →
                                motive ⋯) →
            (t : CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CohG.recOn :

  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
    {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte} →
      {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor} →

            ((metas :
                  ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                    Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) →
                (metas_disj :
                    ∀ (i j : Int) (mci mcj : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                      i ≠ j →

                          Iris.Std.get? mm j = some mcj → CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint mci mcj) →
                  (bytes :

                        Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ k = b) →
                    (cursor_key :
                        ∀ (k : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                          Iris.Std.get? mk k = some c → k = 0) →
                      (cursor :
                          ∀ (c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
                            Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c →
                              c.lastAddr = σ.lastAddress ∧ c.nextId = σ.nextAllocId) →
                        (cur_dead :
                            Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                              ∀ (id : Int),
                                σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
                          (cur_alloc :
                              Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                ∀ (id : Int), σ.nextAllocId ≤ id → σ.allocations.get? id = none) →
                            (cur_meta_lt :
                                Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                  ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                    Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → id < σ.nextAllocId) →

                                  Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                                      Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b → σ.lastAddress ≤ k) →
                                (cur_meta_lo :
                                    Iris.Std.get? mk 0 ≠ none →
                                      ∀ (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                                        Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc → σ.lastAddress ≤ mc.addr) →
                                  motive ⋯) →
              motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CohG.storeRange :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (a : Int) (bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
      (∀ (j : Nat), j < bs'.length → ∃ b, Iris.Std.get? mb (a + ↑j) = some b) →
        CerberusHeapLang.CohG (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ a bs') mm
          (CerberusHeapLang.insertRange mb a bs') mk

∀ (ds ds' : List dyn_annotation) (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds' v) =
    CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge (ds ++ ds') v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge_mk :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M } =
    { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w, ρ := ρ, M := M }
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal →
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.inj :
∀ {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {M_1 : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1, M := M_1 } → w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ M = M_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.injEq :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx)
  (w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (M_1 : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  ({ w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1, M := M_1 }) = (w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ M = M_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
      {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} →
        {w' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
          {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
            {M' : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} →
              { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { w := w', ρ := ρ', M := M' } →
                (w = w' → ρ = ρ' → M = M' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  sizeOf { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M } = 1 + sizeOf w + sizeOf ρ + sizeOf M
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusionType :
Sort u → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Sort u
----

  ((w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
      (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) →
        (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) → motive { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M }) →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) → motive t

  (t : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) →
    ((w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
        (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) →

  (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds v).val = v.val

def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.w :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.ρ :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.ρ_merge :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
  (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds v).ρ = v.ρ

ctor CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.mk :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.mk.inj :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {e_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {M_1 : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { e := e_1, ρ := ρ_1, M := M_1 } → e = e_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ M = M_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.mk.injEq :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx)
  (e_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (M_1 : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  ({ e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { e := e_1, ρ := ρ_1, M := M_1 }) = (e = e_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ M = M_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
      {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} →
        {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
            {M' : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} →
              { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = { e := e', ρ := ρ', M := M' } →
                (e = e' → ρ = ρ' → M = M' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  sizeOf { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = 1 + sizeOf e + sizeOf ρ + sizeOf M
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt.noConfusionType :
Sort u → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt → Sort u
----

  ((e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) →
        (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) → motive { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M }) →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) → motive t

  (t : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) →

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → context → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false →
      (∀ (n : Nat),
          get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
            List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r →
          CerberusHeapLang.Decomp (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.apply_eq :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → apply_ctx ctx r = e
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below :
{motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
    {a_1 : context} → {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.annot :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
  (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
  (hmap :
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
        List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
  (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a → motive b ctx r a → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
          (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
              {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
              (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
              (hmap :
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                    List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
              (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
                (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              motive_1 t t_1
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.rec :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a),
        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
          (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a),
          motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a),
            motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
              {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
              (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
              (hmap :
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                    List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
              (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
                (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),
                motive_1 t t_1

      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_3 a_4),
      CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → motive a a_3 a_4 t) →
    motive a a_1 a_2 t

def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
          (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
            motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
              (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
          (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
              {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
              (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
              (hmap :
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                    List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
            (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                  (Cwseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
              motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.get_ctx_at :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    ∀ (n : Nat), CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ n → get_ctx_lemFuel n e = [(ctx, r)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.get_ctx_default :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel → get_ctx e = [(ctx, r)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.jumpRedex?_eq :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.not_irreducible :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → is_irreducible e = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.rebuild :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? r = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.rec :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive e1 ctx r a →
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
          motive e1 ctx r a →
            motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
              (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
            motive e1 ctx r a →
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
                (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
          (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
              {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
              (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
              (hmap :
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                    List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
              motive b ctx r a →
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
            (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
                motive e1 ctx r a →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    (Cwseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
              ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2), motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.recOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive e1 ctx r a →
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
          motive e1 ctx r a →
            motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
              (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
            motive e1 ctx r a →
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
                (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
          (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
              {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
              (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
              (hmap :
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                    List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
              motive b ctx r a →
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
            (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
                motive e1 ctx r a →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    (Cwseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
              motive a a_1 a_2 t
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.step_factor :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out →
      (∃ r' ρ' σ',
          (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
              r ≠ CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.Step M (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') ∧ out = (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')) ∨
        ∃ ra l pes, r = CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes ∧ CerberusHeapLang.Step M (r, ρ, σ) out
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.step_factor' :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
        r ≠ CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out →
        ∃ r' ρ' σ',
          CerberusHeapLang.Step M (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') ∧ out = (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.toVal_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none
----

  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → is_unseq_with_ccall ctx = false

∀ {v : value} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v_1 : value} {σ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v_1 σ_1 →
    v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1

    (v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1)

  {v : value} →
    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      {v' : value} →
        {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v' σ' →
            (v = v' → σ = σ' → P) → P

  sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ) = 1 + sizeOf v + sizeOf σ

inductive CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch :

  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome → Prop
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.casesOn :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.done :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {v : value},
  e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) →
    CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v)
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.rec :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop},
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
            motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.recOn :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →

            σ)

        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)

              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.refused :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome},
  o.isRefusal →
    (∀ (out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) out) →
      CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ o
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.removeAnnot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) →
    CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ
      (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
        (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)
        σ)
----

∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ
      (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ')

def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatchU.casesOn :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatchU M e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatchU M e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (M.thread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (M.thread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ) σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t

          (ih : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.below a_1) (a_ih : motive e1 a_1)
          (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.below a_2) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_2), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →

          motive_1 t t_1

          motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 a_2 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →

            (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.below t), motive_1 t t_1

∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) ⋯) →
      (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e2),
          motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
        (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable b), motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
          motive a t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.jumpRedex?_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e → CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none

  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe))

rec CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a → Prop},
  (∀ (a : List annot) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) ⋯) →
      (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e2),
          motive e1 a_1 →
            motive e2 a_2 →
              motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
        (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable b),
            motive b a_1 → motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a), motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.recOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable a),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) ⋯) →
      (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e2),
          motive e1 a_1 →
            motive e2 a_2 →
              motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
        (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable b),
            motive b a_1 → motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
          motive a t

ctor CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.sseq :

  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable
        (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))

theorem CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.step_env :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e' ∧ ρ' = ρ

Type

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Frag :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.annot :

  CerberusHeapLang.Frag b → CerberusHeapLang.Frag (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b))
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below :
{motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.annot :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a → motive b a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.case_value :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} {b : List annot}
  {cval : value} {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
  (hbr :
    ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' → CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
  (hbsz :
    ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
          CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)),
  (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation) (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯) →
    (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation) (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
        motive e' ⋯) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a}
  (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below t),
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body)
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive body a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3)
                    (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e2 a)
                    (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e3 a_1),
                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                        (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                              (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                              (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                            (∀ (v1 v2 : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                              (∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                  (hp1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
                                  (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                  (hd1 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                  (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                                    {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                                    (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                    (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                    (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3)
                                    (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                    (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {b : List _root_.annot} {cval : value}
                                      {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                      (hbr :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
                                      (hbsz :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
                                              CerberusHeapLang.esize
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex
                                                  (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats))
                                      (ih :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯)
                                      (hbr_ih :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          motive e' ⋯),
                                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2)
                                        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
                                        (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                      motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.create :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.if_ :

  {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
