rec CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below t → Prop},
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
              motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body)
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive body a),
                  motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3)
                    (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e2 a)
                    (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e3 a_1),
                    motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                        (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
                        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a)
                              (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                              (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
                              motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                            (∀ (v1 v2 : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                              (∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                  (hp1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
                                  (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                  (hd1 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                  (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                                    {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                                    (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                    (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                    (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3)
                                    (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                    (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {b : List _root_.annot} {cval : value}
                                      {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                      (hbr :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
                                      (hbsz :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
                                              CerberusHeapLang.esize
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex
                                                  (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats))
                                      (ih :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯)
                                      (hbr_ih :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          motive e' ⋯),
                                      (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2)
                                        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
                                        (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1)
                                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
                                        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                      ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        {t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a}
                                        (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below t), motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.run :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  (hdep : ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.save :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a → motive body a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.sseq :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} {pa : List annot}
  {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a →
    motive e1 a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1 → motive e2 a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.sseq_spec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} {pa pb : List annot}
  {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a →
    motive e1 a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1 → motive e2 a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.sseq_sym :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} {pa : List annot}
  {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a →
    motive e1 a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1 → motive e2 a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.store :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
  (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.store_op :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
  (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false) (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
  (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
  (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3) (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
  (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel), CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.val_pure :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} (v : value),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below.wseq :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop} {pa : List annot}
  {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a →
    motive e1 a → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below a_1 → motive e2 a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below ⋯
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.brecOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a),
  (∀ (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a),
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag.below t → motive a t) →
    motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.case_value :
∀ {b : List annot} {cval : value} {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)},
  (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' → CerberusHeapLang.Frag e') →
    (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
        select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
          CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
            CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Frag.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a),
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body),
                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3),
                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                        motive
                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                            (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                          ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                              motive
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
                                ⋯) →
                            (∀ (v1 v2 : value), motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals v1 v2) ⋯) →
                              (∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                  (hp1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
                                  (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                  (hd1 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                  (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex PtrEq [pe1, pe2]) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                                    {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                                    (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                    (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                    (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3)
                                    (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                    (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo)
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {b : List _root_.annot} {cval : value}
                                      {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                      (hbr :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
                                      (hbsz :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
                                              CerberusHeapLang.esize
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex
                                                  (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)),
                                      motive
                                        (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)
                                        ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                                        motive
                                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                                            (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                                          ⋯) →
                                      motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.create :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {pref : prefix0},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.decomp :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
      ∃ ctx r, CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r ∧ CerberusHeapLang.Frag r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.esize_le_pot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e → CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ CerberusHeapLang.pot e
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.esize_step_bound :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e + 1 ∨
        ∃ l pes params cont,
          CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) ∧ e' = cont
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.if_ :
∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3 → CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.load :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.load_op :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.memop_op :
∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex PtrEq [pe1, pe2])
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.memop_vals :
∀ (v1 v2 : value), CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals v1 v2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.pot_le_two :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e → CerberusHeapLang.pot e ≤ 2 * CerberusHeapLang.esize e
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.pot_step_bound :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.pot e' ≤ CerberusHeapLang.pot e ∨
        ∃ l pes params cont,
          CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) ∧ e' = cont
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.pure_sym :
∀ {pb : List annot} {x : sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)))
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Frag.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop},
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
              motive e1 a →
                motive e2 a_1 →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b),
                motive b a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body),
                  motive body a → motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3),
                    motive e2 a → motive e3 a_1 → motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                        motive e1 a →
                          motive e2 a_1 →
                            motive
                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                              ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                              motive e1 a →
                                motive e2 a_1 →
                                  motive
                                    (generic_expr.Expr []
                                      (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
                                    ⋯) →
                            (∀ (v1 v2 : value), motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals v1 v2) ⋯) →
                              (∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                  (hp1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
                                  (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                  (hd1 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                  (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex PtrEq [pe1, pe2]) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                                    {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                                    (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                    (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                    (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3)
                                    (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                    (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo)
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {b : List _root_.annot} {cval : value}
                                      {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                      (hbr :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
                                      (hbsz :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
                                              CerberusHeapLang.esize
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex
                                                  (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)),
                                      (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          motive e' ⋯) →
                                        motive
                                          (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval))
                                            pats)
                                          ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                                        motive e1 a →
                                          motive e2 a_1 →
                                            motive
                                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                                (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                                              ⋯) →
                                      ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a), motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Frag.recOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Frag a),
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
              motive e1 a →
                motive e2 a_1 →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag b),
                motive b a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag body),
                  motive body a → motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e3),
                    motive e2 a → motive e3 a_1 → motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                        motive e1 a →
                          motive e2 a_1 →
                            motive
                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                              ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                              motive e1 a →
                                motive e2 a_1 →
                                  motive
                                    (generic_expr.Expr []
                                      (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
                                    ⋯) →
                            (∀ (v1 v2 : value), motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals v1 v2) ⋯) →
                              (∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                  (hp1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
                                  (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                  (hd1 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                  (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex PtrEq [pe1, pe2]) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                                    {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                                    (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                    (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                                    (hp3 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3)
                                    (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                                    (hd3 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo)
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {b : List _root_.annot} {cval : value}
                                      {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                      (hbr :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.Frag e')
                                      (hbsz :
                                        ∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation),
                                          select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
                                            CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤
                                              CerberusHeapLang.esize
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex
                                                  (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)),
                                      (∀ (e' : expr core_run_annotation)
                                          (a : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                          motive e' ⋯) →
                                        motive
                                          (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval))
                                            pats)
                                          ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List _root_.annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (a : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1)
                                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2),
                                        motive e1 a →
                                          motive e2 a_1 →
                                            motive
                                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                                (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                                              ⋯) →
                                      motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.run :
∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.save :
∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag body → CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.sseq :
∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.sseq_spec :
∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag
        (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.sseq_sym :
∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.stateInert_step :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e = true →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
        σ' = σ ∧
          (CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e' = true ∨
            ∃ l pes params cont,
              CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) ∧ e' = cont)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Frag.step :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
    ∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → CerberusHeapLang.Frag e'
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.store :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Frag.store_op :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
      valueFromPexpr pe3 = none →
        CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
          CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3 →
            CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo)
----

  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.Frag e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag (generic_expr.Expr [] (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))

def CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.casesOn :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) →
    ((tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) →
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) →
          (extern : Fmap sym sym) →
            (tid : Nat) →
              (parent : Option Nat) →
                (stack : stack core_run_annotation) →
                  (errno : CerbMem.PointerValue) →
                    (proc : Option sym) →
                      (execLoc : exec_location) →
                        (currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc) →
                          (runState : core_run_state) →
                            motive
                              { tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern, tid := tid,
                                parent := parent, stack := stack, errno := errno, proc := proc,
                                execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
                                runState := runState }) →
      motive t

theorem CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.labels_eq_of_proc :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {p : sym},
  M.proc = some p →
    M.labels =
      match
        fmapLookupBy (fun s1 s2 => Lem_Basic_classes.ordCompare s1 s2) (M.resolveProc p)
          M.runState.labeled with
      | some Q => Q
      | none => fmapEmpty
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.labels_lookup_some :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {l : sym}
  {pc : List (sym × core_base_type) × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some pc →
    ∃ p, M.proc = some p ∧ CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt M.runState (M.resolveProc p) M.labels
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.mk :
Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition) →
  generic_file Unit core_run_annotation →
    Fmap sym sym →
      Nat →
        Option Nat →
          stack core_run_annotation →
            CerbMem.PointerValue →
              Option sym →
                exec_location → CerbLocation.Loc → core_run_state → CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.mk.inj :
∀ {tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)}
  {file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation} {extern : Fmap sym sym} {tid : Nat}
  {parent : Option Nat} {stack : stack core_run_annotation} {errno : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {proc : Option sym} {execLoc : exec_location} {currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {runState : core_run_state} {tagDefs_1 : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)}
  {file_1 : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation} {extern_1 : Fmap sym sym} {tid_1 : Nat}
  {parent_1 : Option Nat} {stack_1 : _root_.stack core_run_annotation}
  {errno_1 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {proc_1 : Option sym} {execLoc_1 : exec_location}
  {currentLoc_1 : CerbLocation.Loc} {runState_1 : core_run_state},
  { tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern, tid := tid, parent := parent,
        stack := stack, errno := errno, proc := proc, execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
        runState := runState } =
      { tagDefs := tagDefs_1, file := file_1, extern := extern_1, tid := tid_1, parent := parent_1,
        stack := stack_1, errno := errno_1, proc := proc_1, execLoc := execLoc_1,
        currentLoc := currentLoc_1, runState := runState_1 } →
    tagDefs = tagDefs_1 ∧
      file = file_1 ∧
        extern = extern_1 ∧
          tid = tid_1 ∧
            parent = parent_1 ∧
              stack = stack_1 ∧
                errno = errno_1 ∧
                  proc = proc_1 ∧
                    execLoc = execLoc_1 ∧ currentLoc = currentLoc_1 ∧ runState = runState_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.mk.injEq :
∀ (tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (extern : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
  (parent : Option Nat) (stack : stack core_run_annotation) (errno : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (proc : Option sym) (execLoc : exec_location) (currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (runState : core_run_state) (tagDefs_1 : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (file_1 : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (extern_1 : Fmap sym sym) (tid_1 : Nat)
  (parent_1 : Option Nat) (stack_1 : _root_.stack core_run_annotation)
  (errno_1 : CerbMem.PointerValue) (proc_1 : Option sym) (execLoc_1 : exec_location)
  (currentLoc_1 : CerbLocation.Loc) (runState_1 : core_run_state),
  ({ tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern, tid := tid, parent := parent,
        stack := stack, errno := errno, proc := proc, execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
        runState := runState } =
      { tagDefs := tagDefs_1, file := file_1, extern := extern_1, tid := tid_1, parent := parent_1,
        stack := stack_1, errno := errno_1, proc := proc_1, execLoc := execLoc_1,
        currentLoc := currentLoc_1, runState := runState_1 }) =
    (tagDefs = tagDefs_1 ∧
      file = file_1 ∧
        extern = extern_1 ∧
          tid = tid_1 ∧
            parent = parent_1 ∧
              stack = stack_1 ∧
                errno = errno_1 ∧
                  proc = proc_1 ∧
                    execLoc = execLoc_1 ∧ currentLoc = currentLoc_1 ∧ runState = runState_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)} →
    {file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation} →
      {extern : Fmap sym sym} →
        {tid : Nat} →
          {parent : Option Nat} →
            {stack : stack core_run_annotation} →
              {errno : CerbMem.PointerValue} →
                {proc : Option sym} →
                  {execLoc : exec_location} →
                    {currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc} →
                      {runState : core_run_state} →
                        {tagDefs' : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)} →
                          {file' : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation} →
                            {extern' : Fmap sym sym} →
                              {tid' : Nat} →
                                {parent' : Option Nat} →
                                  {stack' : _root_.stack core_run_annotation} →
                                    {errno' : CerbMem.PointerValue} →
                                      {proc' : Option sym} →
                                        {execLoc' : exec_location} →
                                          {currentLoc' : CerbLocation.Loc} →
                                            {runState' : core_run_state} →
                                              { tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern,
                                                    tid := tid, parent := parent, stack := stack,
                                                    errno := errno, proc := proc,
                                                    execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
                                                    runState := runState } =
                                                  { tagDefs := tagDefs', file := file',
                                                    extern := extern', tid := tid',
                                                    parent := parent', stack := stack',
                                                    errno := errno', proc := proc',
                                                    execLoc := execLoc', currentLoc := currentLoc',
                                                    runState := runState' } →
                                                (tagDefs = tagDefs' →
                                                    file = file' →
                                                      extern = extern' →
                                                        tid = tid' →
                                                          parent = parent' →
                                                            stack = stack' →
                                                              errno = errno' →
                                                                proc = proc' →
                                                                  execLoc = execLoc' →
                                                                    currentLoc = currentLoc' →
                                                                      runState = runState' → P) →
                                                  P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (extern : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
  (parent : Option Nat) (stack : stack core_run_annotation) (errno : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (proc : Option sym) (execLoc : exec_location) (currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (runState : core_run_state),
  sizeOf
      { tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern, tid := tid, parent := parent,
        stack := stack, errno := errno, proc := proc, execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
        runState := runState } =
    1 + sizeOf tagDefs + sizeOf file + sizeOf extern + sizeOf tid + sizeOf parent + sizeOf stack +
              sizeOf errno +
            sizeOf proc +
          sizeOf execLoc +
        sizeOf currentLoc +
      sizeOf runState
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx.noConfusionType P t t'
----

        (extern : Fmap sym sym) →
          (tid : Nat) →

                      (currentLoc : CerbLocation.Loc) →
                        (runState : core_run_state) →
                          motive
                            { tagDefs := tagDefs, file := file, extern := extern, tid := tid,
                              parent := parent, stack := stack, errno := errno, proc := proc,
                              execLoc := execLoc, currentLoc := currentLoc,
                              runState := runState }) →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) → motive t

inductive CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell →
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell →
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.byte_all :
∀ {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb →
    ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
        ∀ (j : Nat), j < c.bytes.length → Iris.Std.get? mb (c.addr + ↑j) = c.bytes[j]?
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.byte_cov :
∀ {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb →
    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
      Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b →
        ∃ i c,
          Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧
            c.addr ≤ k ∧
              k < c.addr + ↑c.bytes.length ∧
                b = c.bytes[(k - c.addr).toNat]?.getD CerberusHeapLang.undefByte
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.casesOn :
{m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
    {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb) →
          ((meta_sub :
                ∀ (i : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                  Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mc →
                    ∃ c, Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧ mc = CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c) →
              (meta_all :
                  ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                    Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
                      Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)) →
                (byte_cov :
                    ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                      Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b →
                        ∃ i c,
                          Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧
                            c.addr ≤ k ∧
                              k < c.addr + ↑c.bytes.length ∧
                                b = c.bytes[(k - c.addr).toNat]?.getD CerberusHeapLang.undefByte) →
                  (byte_all :
                      ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                        Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
                          ∀ (j : Nat),
                            j < c.bytes.length → Iris.Std.get? mb (c.addr + ↑j) = c.bytes[j]?) →
                    motive ⋯) →
            motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.cohG :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb → CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb ∅
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.meta_all :
∀ {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb →
    ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m i = some c → Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.meta_sub :
∀ {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb →
    ∀ (i : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
      Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mc →
        ∃ c, Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧ mc = CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.mk :
∀ {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell}
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte},
  (∀ (i : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
      Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mc →
        ∃ c, Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧ mc = CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c) →
    (∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        Iris.Std.get? m i = some c → Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)) →
      (∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
          Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b →
            ∃ i c,
              Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧
                c.addr ≤ k ∧
                  k < c.addr + ↑c.bytes.length ∧
                    b = c.bytes[(k - c.addr).toNat]?.getD CerberusHeapLang.undefByte) →
        (∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
            Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
              ∀ (j : Nat), j < c.bytes.length → Iris.Std.get? mb (c.addr + ↑j) = c.bytes[j]?) →
          CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf.rec :
{m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
    {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb → Sort u} →
        ((meta_sub :
              ∀ (i : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
                Iris.Std.get? mm i = some mc →
                  ∃ c, Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧ mc = CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c) →
            (meta_all :
                ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
                    Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)) →
              (byte_cov :
                  ∀ (k : Int) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte),
                    Iris.Std.get? mb k = some b →
                      ∃ i c,
                        Iris.Std.get? m i = some c ∧
                          c.addr ≤ k ∧
                            k < c.addr + ↑c.bytes.length ∧
                              b = c.bytes[(k - c.addr).toNat]?.getD CerberusHeapLang.undefByte) →
                (byte_all :
                    ∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                      Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
                        ∀ (j : Nat),
                          j < c.bytes.length → Iris.Std.get? mb (c.addr + ↑j) = c.bytes[j]?) →
                  motive ⋯) →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.MetaByteOf m mm mb) → motive t
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.mk.inj :
∀ {addr : Int} {ty : ctype} {addr_1 : Int} {ty_1 : ctype},
  { addr := addr, ty := ty } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1 } → addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.mk.injEq :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype) (addr_1 : Int) (ty_1 : ctype),
  ({ addr := addr, ty := ty } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1 }) = (addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {addr : Int} →
    {ty : ctype} →
      {addr' : Int} →
        {ty' : ctype} →
          { addr := addr, ty := ty } = { addr := addr', ty := ty' } →
            (addr = addr' → ty = ty' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype), sizeOf { addr := addr, ty := ty } = 1 + sizeOf addr + sizeOf ty
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell.noConfusionType P t t'

inductive CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Int → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.alloc :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc →
    ∃ al,
      σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
        al.base = mc.addr ∧
          al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype mc.ty) ∧
            al.ty = some mc.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = mc.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype mc.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some mc.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy mc.ty = false) → motive ⋯) →
            motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.dead :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false →
    (∃ al,
        σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
          al.base = mc.addr ∧
            al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype mc.ty) ∧
              al.ty = some mc.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
      CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy mc.ty = false → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.nonAtomic :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc → CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy mc.ty = false
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc → Sort u} →
        ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
            (alloc :
                ∃ al,
                  σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                    al.base = mc.addr ∧
                      al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype mc.ty) ∧
                        al.ty = some mc.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
              (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy mc.ty = false) → motive ⋯) →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.recOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc → Sort u} →

                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy mc.ty = false) → motive ⋯) →
            motive t

theorem CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh.writeBytes :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id mc →
    ∀ (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),

theorem CerberusHeapLang.PMunion_get? :
∀ {V : Type} (m₁ m₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF V) (k : Int),
  Iris.Std.get? (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union m₁ m₂) k =
    (Iris.Std.get? m₁ k).orElse fun x => Iris.Std.get? m₂ k

inductive CerberusHeapLang.PePure :
generic_pexpr Unit sym → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.PePure.arrayShift :
∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 → CerberusHeapLang.PePure (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2))
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below :
{motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop} →
  {a : generic_pexpr Unit sym} → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.arrayShift :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop} (a : List annot)
  (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2),
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1 →
    motive pe1 a_1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2 → motive pe2 a_2 → CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : generic_pexpr Unit sym} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below t → Prop}
  {a : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a}
  (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below t),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (x : sym), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
          (ih : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1) (a_ih : motive pe1 a_1)
          (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2) (a_ih_1 : motive pe2 a_2), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1) (a_ih : motive pe1 a_1)
            (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2) (a_ih_1 : motive pe2 a_2), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.op :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop} (a : List annot)
  (op : binop) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1)
  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2),
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1 →
    motive pe1 a_1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2 → motive pe2 a_2 → CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below ⋯
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : generic_pexpr Unit sym} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below t → Prop},
  (∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (x : sym), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
          (ih : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1) (a_ih : motive pe1 a_1)
          (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2) (a_ih_1 : motive pe2 a_2),
          motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 a_2 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_1) (a_ih : motive pe1 a_1)
            (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below a_2) (a_ih_1 : motive pe2 a_2),
            motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 a_2 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym} {t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a}
            (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below t), motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.sym :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop} (a : List annot)
  (x : sym), CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below.val :
∀ {motive : (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.PePure a → Prop} (a : List annot)
  (v : value), CerberusHeapLang.PePure.below ⋯
----

    motive a t

def CerberusHeapLang.PePure.casesOn :

  {a : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), motive (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (x : sym), motive (Pexpr a () (PEsym x)) ⋯) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2),
          motive (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) ⋯) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}

            motive (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) ⋯) →
          motive a t

rec CerberusHeapLang.PePure.rec :

  (∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), motive (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) ⋯) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (x : sym), motive (Pexpr a () (PEsym x)) ⋯) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
          (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2),
          motive pe1 a_1 → motive pe2 a_2 → motive (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) ⋯) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym}
            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe1) (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2),
            motive pe1 a_1 → motive pe2 a_2 → motive (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : generic_pexpr Unit _root_.sym} (t : CerberusHeapLang.PePure a), motive a t

def CerberusHeapLang.PePure.recOn :

          motive a t

  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Redex
    (generic_expr.Expr []
      (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
        (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))

  CerberusHeapLang.Redex
    (generic_expr.Expr []

  CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex pe pats)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Redex.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Redex a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Redex a),
  (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
      {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
      (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
          {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr []
                (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2))
              ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr []
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))
                ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (hirr :
                  is_irreducible
                      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
                    false),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b))))
                  ⋯) →
              (∀ (sb : sym × core_base_type)
                  (ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym))
                  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
                      (pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex pe pats) ⋯) →
                    (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                        motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none),
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex pe) ⋯) →
                        (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                              motive
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                ⋯) →
                            (∀ (mop : memop) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                                motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop pes) ⋯) →
                              (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                                  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                                  (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                    {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal}
                                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                    motive
                                      (generic_expr.Expr []
                                        (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty)
                                          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                      motive
                                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                                          (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                            (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
                                            e2))
                                        ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                                        {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                        motive
                                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                                            (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v))
                                              e2))
                                          ⋯) →
                                      motive a t
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Redex.jumpRedex?_some_inv :
∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.Redex r →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? r = some (l, pes) → ∃ ra, r = CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.pure_e :
