  valueFromPexpr pe = none → CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex pe)
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Redex.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Redex a → Prop},
  (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
      {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
      (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
          {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr []
                (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2))
              ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr []
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))
                ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (hirr :
                  is_irreducible
                      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
                    false),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b))))
                  ⋯) →
              (∀ (sb : sym × core_base_type)
                  (ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym))
                  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
                      (pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex pe pats) ⋯) →
                    (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                        motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none),
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex pe) ⋯) →
                        (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                              motive
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                ⋯) →
                            (∀ (mop : memop) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                                motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop pes) ⋯) →
                              (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                                  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                                  (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                    {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal}
                                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                    motive
                                      (generic_expr.Expr []
                                        (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty)
                                          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                      motive
                                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                                          (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                            (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
                                            e2))
                                        ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                                        {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                        motive
                                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                                            (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v))
                                              e2))
                                          ⋯) →
                                      ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        (t : CerberusHeapLang.Redex a), motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Redex.recOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Redex a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Redex a),
  (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
      {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
      (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
          {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr []
                (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2))
              ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr []
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))
                ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (hirr :
                  is_irreducible
                      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
                    false),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b))))
                  ⋯) →
              (∀ (sb : sym × core_base_type)
                  (ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym))
                  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
                      (pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex pe pats) ⋯) →
                    (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                        motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none),
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex pe) ⋯) →
                        (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                              {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                              motive
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                ⋯) →
                            (∀ (mop : memop) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
                                motive (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop pes) ⋯) →
                              (∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
                                  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order)
                                  (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none),
                                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) ⋯) →
                                (∀ {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                    {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal}
                                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                    motive
                                      (generic_expr.Expr []
                                        (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty)
                                          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) e2))
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                      motive
                                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                                          (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                            (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
                                            e2))
                                        ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
                                        {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                                        {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
                                        motive
                                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                                            (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                                              (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                                (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v))
                                              e2))
                                          ⋯) →
                                      motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.run :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.save :
∀ (sb : sym × core_base_type)
  (ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr), CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.store :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.store_op :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} (mo : memory_order),
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.Redex (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.wbeta_annot :
∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Redex
    (generic_expr.Expr []
      (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
        (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Redex.wbeta_pure :
∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Redex
    (generic_expr.Expr []
      (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
        (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2))
----

∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m m' : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap},
  CerberusHeapLang.Sat σ m → m' ⊆ m → CerberusHeapLang.Sat σ m'

        (ty : ctype) →
          (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) → motive { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes }) →

ctor CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk :
Int → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.inj :
∀ {addr : Int} {ty : ctype} {bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte} {addr_1 : Int} {ty_1 : ctype}
  {bytes_1 : List CerbMem.AbsByte},
  { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1, bytes := bytes_1 } →
    addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1 ∧ bytes = bytes_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.injEq :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype) (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (addr_1 : Int) (ty_1 : ctype)
  (bytes_1 : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  ({ addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1, bytes := bytes_1 }) =
    (addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1 ∧ bytes = bytes_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {addr : Int} →
    {ty : ctype} →
      {bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte} →
        {addr' : Int} →
          {ty' : ctype} →
            {bytes' : List CerbMem.AbsByte} →
              { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } =
                  { addr := addr', ty := ty', bytes := bytes' } →
                (addr = addr' → ty = ty' → bytes = bytes' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype) (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  sizeOf { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = 1 + sizeOf addr + sizeOf ty + sizeOf bytes
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusionType :

  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →

      Iris.genHeapGS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
        Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
          Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
            CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.mk.noConfusion :
{hlc : outParam Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    {P : Sort u} →
      {invGS : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF} →
        {byteGS : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
          {metaGS : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
            {cursorGS :
                Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
              {invGS' : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF} →
                {byteGS' : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                  {metaGS' :
                      Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                    {cursorGS' :
                        Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF
                          CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                      { invGS := invGS, byteGS := byteGS, metaGS := metaGS, cursorGS := cursorGS } =
                          { invGS := invGS', byteGS := byteGS', metaGS := metaGS',
                            cursorGS := cursorGS' } →
                        (invGS ≍ invGS' →
                            byteGS ≍ byteGS' → metaGS ≍ metaGS' → cursorGS ≍ cursorGS' → P) →
                          P
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
    {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
      {t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF} →
        {hlc' : Iris.HasLC} →
          {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
            {t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc' GF'} →
              hlc = hlc' → GF = GF' → t ≍ t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.noConfusionType P t t'
----

Sort u →
  {hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
    {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF →
        {hlc' : Iris.HasLC} →
          {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc' GF' → Sort u
----

              (cursorGS :
                  Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
                motive
                  { invGS := invGS, byteGS := byteGS, metaGS := metaGS, cursorGS := cursorGS }) →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF) → motive t

      Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
        Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
          CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF

def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.mk.noConfusion :
{GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
  {P : Sort u} →
    {toInvGpreS : Iris.InvGpreS GF} →
      {byte_pre : Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
        {meta_pre : Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
          {cursor_pre :
              Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
            {toInvGpreS' : Iris.InvGpreS GF} →
              {byte_pre' : Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                {meta_pre' :
                    Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                  {cursor_pre' :
                      Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF
                        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
                    { toInvGpreS := toInvGpreS, byte_pre := byte_pre, meta_pre := meta_pre,
                          cursor_pre := cursor_pre } =
                        { toInvGpreS := toInvGpreS', byte_pre := byte_pre', meta_pre := meta_pre',
                          cursor_pre := cursor_pre' } →
                      (toInvGpreS ≍ toInvGpreS' →
                          byte_pre ≍ byte_pre' →
                            meta_pre ≍ meta_pre' → cursor_pre ≍ cursor_pre' → P) →
                        P
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    {t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF} →
      {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
        {t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF'} →
          GF = GF' → t ≍ t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.noConfusionType :
Sort u →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF →
      {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF' → Sort u
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.rec :
{GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
  {motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF → Sort u} →
    ([toInvGpreS : Iris.InvGpreS GF] →
        (byte_pre : Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
          (meta_pre :
              Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
            (cursor_pre :
                Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
              motive
                { toInvGpreS := toInvGpreS, byte_pre := byte_pre, meta_pre := meta_pre,
                  cursor_pre := cursor_pre }) →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.recOn :
{GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
  {motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF → Sort u} →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF) →
      ([toInvGpreS : Iris.InvGpreS GF] →
          (byte_pre : Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerbMem.AbsByte GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
            (meta_pre :
                Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
              (cursor_pre :
                  Iris.genHeapPreS Int CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor GF
                    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
                motive
                  { toInvGpreS := toInvGpreS, byte_pre := byte_pre, meta_pre := meta_pre,
                    cursor_pre := cursor_pre }) →
        motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS.toInvGpreS :
{GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} → [self : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] → Iris.InvGpreS GF
----

ctor CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot :
List dyn_annotation → value → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal

def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.elim :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
    t.ctorIdx = 1 →
      ((ds : List dyn_annotation) → (v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) →
        motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.inj :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {ds_1 : List dyn_annotation} {v_1 : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds_1 v_1 →
    ds = ds_1 ∧ v = v_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.injEq :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (ds_1 : List dyn_annotation) (v_1 : value),
  (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds_1 v_1) =
    (ds = ds_1 ∧ v = v_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} →
    {v : value} →
      {ds' : List dyn_annotation} →
        {v' : value} →
          CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds' v' →
            (ds = ds' → v = v' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value),
  sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) = 1 + sizeOf ds + sizeOf v
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.casesOn :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
    ((v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) →
      ((ds : List dyn_annotation) → (v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) →
        motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.ctorElim :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} →
  (ctorIdx : Nat) →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
      ctorIdx = t.ctorIdx → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.ctorElimType ctorIdx → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.ctorElimType :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} → Nat → Sort (max 1 u)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.ctorIdx :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Nat
----

CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal

theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge_merge :
∀ (ds ds' : List dyn_annotation) (v : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds' v) =
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge (ds ++ ds') v

def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.noConfusionType P t t'

  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
    t.ctorIdx = 0 → ((v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) → motive t

theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure.inj :
∀ {v v_1 : value}, CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v_1 → v = v_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure.injEq :
∀ (v v_1 : value),
  (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v_1) = (v = v_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {v v' : value} →
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v' → (v = v' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (v : value), sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) = 1 + sizeOf v
----

    ((ds : List dyn_annotation) → (v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) → motive t

  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →

def CerberusHeapLang.StateInertLabels :
CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx → Prop

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Step :
CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem →
    CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_ctx :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
  {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ) out →
    (CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false ∧
        CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none ∧
          ∃ b' ρ' σ',
            CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') ∧
              out = (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ')) ∨
      (∃ a2 ds2 c,
          b = generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 c) ∧
            out = (generic_expr.Expr (a ++ a2) (Eannot (ds ++ ds2) c), ρ, σ)) ∨
        ∃ l pes params cont vs ev0 evs,
          CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = some (l, pes) ∧
              ρ = ev0 :: evs ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern ρ pes = some vs ∧
                    out = (cont, CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs ρ, σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_merge :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M
    (generic_expr.Expr a1 (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 b))), ρ, σ)
    (generic_expr.Expr (a1 ++ a2) (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b), ρ, σ)
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Step.below :
{M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} →
  {motive :
      (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop} →
    {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.annot_ctx :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2 →
    motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_2 → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.annot_merge :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.case_value :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
  {cval : value} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (hv : valueFromPexpr pe = some cval)
  (hsel : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'), CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.casesOn :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1) → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t),
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {ov : object_value}
                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                    {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                      {ds : List dyn_annotation} {ov : object_value}
                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                      {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value}
                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                        (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none)
                        (hv : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
                          {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
                          {mo : memory_order} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                          {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                          (hv2 :
                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                              some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))),
                          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                            {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                            (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2)
                            (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_2), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern}
                              {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                              {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                              (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                              (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2)
                              (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_2), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                            (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
                                {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none)
                                (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                                (a_3 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ'))
                                (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_3)
                                (a_ih : motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_3), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                              (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                                  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                (∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {l : sym}
                                    {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                    {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
                                    {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
                                    {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                    (hj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes))
                                    (hl :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont))
                                    (hvs :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern (ev0 :: evs) pes =
                                        some vs),
                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {a : List annot} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                                      {ps :
                                        List
                                          (sym ×
                                            (core_base_type ×
                                                Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                                              generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                      {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {cvals : List value}
                                      {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                      {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                      (hvals :
                                        valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) =
                                          some cvals),
                                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                        {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                        (hg : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue),
                                        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                      (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                          {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                          {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                          (hg :
                                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse),
                                          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                            {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                            {cval : value} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                            {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                            {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                            (hv : valueFromPexpr pe = some cval)
                                            (hsel : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                              {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                              {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                            (∀ {a : List annot} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {b : Bool}
                                                {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                (h1 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe1 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)))
                                                (h2 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe2 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv2)))
                                                (hmem :
                                                  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
                                                      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ =
                                                    some (b, σ')),
                                                motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                              (∀ {a : List annot} {mop : memop}
                                                  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v1 v2 : value}
                                                  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                                  (hv1 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 =
                                                      some v1)
                                                  (hv2 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                      some v2),
                                                  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                                (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
                                                    {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
                                                    {ty : ctype} {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                    {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
                                                    {mo : memory_order}
                                                    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                                    (hv2 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                        some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
                                                    (hv3 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 =
                                                        some cv),
                                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                                  motive_1 t t_1
----

    CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
      some (pv, σ')),

rec CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.rec :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1) → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → Prop},
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {ov : object_value}
                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                    {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                      {ds : List dyn_annotation} {ov : object_value}
                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                      {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value}
                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                        (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none)
                        (hv : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
                          {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
                          {mo : memory_order} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                          {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                          (hv2 :
                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                              some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))),
                          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                            {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                            (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                            (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                            (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_1)
                            (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_1),
                            motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern}
                              {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                              {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                              (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                              (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_1)
                              (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_1),
                              motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                            (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
                                {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none)
                                (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                                (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ'))
                                (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2)
                                (a_ih : motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_2),
                                motive_1 a_2 ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                              (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                                  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                (∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {l : sym}
                                    {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                    {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
                                    {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
                                    {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                    (hj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes))
                                    (hl :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont))
                                    (hvs :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern (ev0 :: evs) pes =
                                        some vs),
                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {a : List annot} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                                      {ps :
                                        List
                                          (sym ×
                                            (core_base_type ×
                                                Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                                              generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                      {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {cvals : List value}
                                      {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                      {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                      (hvals :
                                        valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) =
                                          some cvals),
                                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                        {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                        (hg : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue),
                                        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                      (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                          {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                          {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                          (hg :
                                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse),
                                          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                            {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                            {cval : value} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                            {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                            {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                            (hv : valueFromPexpr pe = some cval)
                                            (hsel : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                              {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                              {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                            (∀ {a : List annot} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {b : Bool}
                                                {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                (h1 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe1 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)))
                                                (h2 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe2 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv2)))
                                                (hmem :
                                                  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
                                                      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ =
                                                    some (b, σ')),
                                                motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                              (∀ {a : List annot} {mop : memop}
                                                  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v1 v2 : value}
                                                  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                                  (hv1 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 =
                                                      some v1)
                                                  (hv2 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                      some v2),
                                                  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                                (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
                                                    {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
                                                    {ty : ctype} {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                    {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
                                                    {mo : memory_order}
                                                    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                                    (hv2 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                        some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
                                                    (hv3 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 =
                                                        some cv),
                                                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                                                  ∀
                                                    {a a_1 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr ×
                                                        CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack ×
                                                          CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                    {t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1}
                                                    (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t),
                                                    motive_1 t t_1
----

      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.sseq_pure :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.sseq_spec_annot :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
  {ov : object_value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----

∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {ov : object_value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
