  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.store :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
  {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
  (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
  (hmv : memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
  (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.store_eval :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
  (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
  (hv2 : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
  (hv3 : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 = some cv), CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.wseq_annot :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.wseq_ctx :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_1 →
    motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.wseq_pure :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.brecOn :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1),
  (∀ (a a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_2), CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → motive a a_2 t) →
    motive a a_1 t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.case_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats), ρ, σ) out →
    ∃ cval e',
      valueFromPexpr pe = some cval ∧
        select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' ∧ out = (e', ρ, σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.case_value :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)} {cval : value}
  {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe = some cval →
    select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats), ρ, σ) (e', ρ, σ)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Step.casesOn :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step M a a_1),
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive
        (generic_expr.Expr a
            (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Store0 lk pe1 pe2 pe3 mo)))),
          ρ, σ)
        (generic_expr.Expr []
            (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp] (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit))))),
          ρ, σ')
        ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Load0 pe1 pe2 mo)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
            ρ, σ')
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive
            (generic_expr.Expr a
                (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Create pe1 pe2 pref)))),
              ρ, σ)
            (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
            ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr a
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
                ev0 :: evs, σ)
              (e2, ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr a
                    (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
                  ev0 :: evs, σ)
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
            (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
                {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                motive
                  (generic_expr.Expr a
                      (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                        (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
                    ev0 :: evs, σ)
                  (e2, ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
              (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
                  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                  motive
                    (generic_expr.Expr a
                        (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
                      ev0 :: evs, σ)
                    (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
                (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {ov : object_value}
                    {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                    {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                    motive
                      (generic_expr.Expr a
                          (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                            (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                              (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure (Vloaded (LVspecified ov))))
                            e2),
                        ev0 :: evs, σ)
                      (e2,
                        update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov))
                          (ev0 :: evs),
                        σ)
                      ⋯) →
                  (∀ {a pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                      {ds : List dyn_annotation} {ov : object_value}
                      {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
                      {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                      motive
                        (generic_expr.Expr a
                            (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                              (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds (Vloaded (LVspecified ov))))
                              e2),
                          ev0 :: evs, σ)
                        (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2),
                          update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty)
                            (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) (ev0 :: evs),
                          σ)
                        ⋯) →
                    (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value}
                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                        (hnv : valueFromPexpr pe = none)
                        (hv : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v),
                        motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe), ρ, σ)
                          (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v))), ρ, σ) ⋯) →
                      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
                          {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
                          {mo : memory_order} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                          {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                          (hv2 :
                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                              some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))),
                          motive
                            (generic_expr.Expr a
                                (Eaction
                                  (Paction polarity.Pos
                                    (Action loc ann
                                      (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pe2 mo)))),
                              ρ, σ)
                            (generic_expr.Expr a
                                (Eaction
                                  (Paction polarity.Pos
                                    (Action loc ann
                                      (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                                        (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))),
                              ρ, σ)
                            ⋯) →
                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                            {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                            (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ')),
                            motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2), ρ, σ)
                              (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1' e2), ρ', σ') ⋯) →
                          (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern}
                              {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                              {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                              (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = none)
                              (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ')),
                              motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1 e2), ρ, σ)
                                (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1' e2), ρ', σ') ⋯) →
                            (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation}
                                {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                (hnj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b = none)
                                (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                                (a_3 : CerberusHeapLang.Step M (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ')),
                                motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ)
                                  (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ') ⋯) →
                              (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                                  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                                  motive
                                    (generic_expr.Expr a1
                                        (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 b))),
                                      ρ, σ)
                                    (generic_expr.Expr (a1 ++ a2) (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b), ρ, σ)
                                    ⋯) →
                                (∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {l : sym}
                                    {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                    {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
                                    {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
                                    {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                    (hj : CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes))
                                    (hl :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont))
                                    (hvs :
                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern (ev0 :: evs) pes =
                                        some vs),
                                    motive (e, ev0 :: evs, σ)
                                      (cont, CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs (ev0 :: evs), σ)
                                      ⋯) →
                                  (∀ {a : List annot} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                                      {ps :
                                        List
                                          (sym ×
                                            (core_base_type ×
                                                Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                                              generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                                      {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {cvals : List value}
                                      {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                      {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                      (hvals :
                                        valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) =
                                          some cvals),
                                      motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esave sb ps body), ev0 :: evs, σ)
                                        (body,
                                          CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs), σ)
                                        ⋯) →
                                    (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                        {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                        (hg : CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue),
                                        motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3), ρ, σ) (e2, ρ, σ)
                                          ⋯) →
                                      (∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                          {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                          {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                          (hg :
                                            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse),
                                          motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3), ρ, σ)
                                            (e3, ρ, σ) ⋯) →
                                        (∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                            {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)}
                                            {cval : value} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                            {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                            {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                            (hv : valueFromPexpr pe = some cval)
                                            (hsel : select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e'),
                                            motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats), ρ, σ)
                                              (e', ρ, σ) ⋯) →
                                          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                                              {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                                              {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
                                              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                                              motive
                                                (generic_expr.Expr a
                                                    (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty)
                                                      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal
                                                        (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
                                                      e2),
                                                  ev0 :: evs, σ)
                                                (e2,
                                                  update_env (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) v
                                                    (ev0 :: evs),
                                                  σ)
                                                ⋯) →
                                            (∀ {a : List annot} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {b : Bool}
                                                {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                (h1 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe1 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)))
                                                (h2 :
                                                  valueFromPexpr pe2 =
                                                    some (Vobject (OVpointer pv2)))
                                                (hmem :
                                                  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
                                                      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ =
                                                    some (b, σ')),
                                                motive
                                                  (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop PtrEq [pe1, pe2]), ρ,
                                                    σ)
                                                  (generic_expr.Expr []
                                                      (Epure
                                                        (Pexpr [] ()
                                                          (PEval (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)))),
                                                    ρ, σ')
                                                  ⋯) →
                                              (∀ {a : List annot} {mop : memop}
                                                  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v1 v2 : value}
                                                  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                  (hnv : valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none)
                                                  (hv1 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 =
                                                      some v1)
                                                  (hv2 :
                                                    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                      some v2),
                                                  motive
                                                    (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop [pe1, pe2]), ρ,
                                                      σ)
                                                    (generic_expr.Expr a
                                                        (Ememop mop
                                                          [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1),
                                                            Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)]),
                                                      ρ, σ)
                                                    ⋯) →
                                                (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
                                                    {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
                                                    {ty : ctype} {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
                                                    {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
                                                    {mo : memory_order}
                                                    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                                                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                                                    (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none)
                                                    (hnv3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = none)
                                                    (hv2 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 =
                                                        some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
                                                    (hv3 :
                                                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 =
                                                        some cv),
                                                    motive
                                                      (generic_expr.Expr a
                                                          (Eaction
                                                            (Paction polarity.Pos
                                                              (Action loc ann
                                                                (Store0 lk
                                                                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                                                                  pe2 pe3 mo)))),
                                                        ρ, σ)
                                                      (generic_expr.Expr a
                                                          (Eaction
                                                            (Paction polarity.Pos
                                                              (Action loc ann
                                                                (Store0 lk
                                                                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                                                                  (Pexpr [] ()
                                                                    (PEval
                                                                      (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))
                                                                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval cv)) mo)))),
                                                        ρ, σ)
                                                      ⋯) →
                                                  motive a a_1 t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.create :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
  {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty) →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
          some (pv, σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M
          (generic_expr.Expr a
              (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Create pe1 pe2 pref)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.create_canonical :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ = some (pv, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))),
        ρ, σ)
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.create_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))),
        ρ, σ)
      out →
    ∃ pv σ',
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
          some (pv, σ') ∧
        out = (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.env_cons :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ev0 :: evs, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → ∃ ev0', ρ' = ev0' :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.env_cons' :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {c c' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M c c' →
    ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
      c.snd.fst = ev0 :: evs → ∃ ev0', c'.snd.fst = ev0' :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.env_invariant_frag :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e → CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → ρ' = ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.esize_succ :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e + 1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.if_false :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3), ρ, σ) (e3, ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.if_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3), ρ, σ) out →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue ∧ out = (e2, ρ, σ) ∨
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse ∧ out = (e3, ρ, σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.if_true :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3), ρ, σ) (e2, ρ, σ)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.jumpRedex?_none_of_empty :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.labels = CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl →
    ∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out → CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.jumpRedex?_none_of_spikeCtx :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (e, ρ, σ) out →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.jump_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {l : sym}
  {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out →
      ∃ params cont vs ev0 evs,
        ρ = ev0 :: evs ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern ρ pes = some vs ∧
              out = (cont, CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs ρ, σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.load :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Load0 pe1 pe2 mo)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
            ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.load_canonical :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
  {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))),
        ρ, σ)
      (generic_expr.Expr []
          (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
            (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
        ρ, σ')
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.load_eval :

  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}

    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M
        (generic_expr.Expr a
            (Eaction

                (Action loc ann (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pe2 mo)))),

        (generic_expr.Expr a
            (Eaction
              (Paction polarity.Pos
                (Action loc ann
                  (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                    (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))),
          ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.load_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step M
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))),
        ρ, σ)
      out →
    ∃ fp mval σ',
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') ∧
        out =
          (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
            ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.load_op_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}

  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M
        (generic_expr.Expr a
            (Eaction
              (Paction polarity.Pos
                (Action loc ann (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pe2 mo)))),
          ρ, σ)
        out →
      ∃ pv,
        CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) ∧
          out =
            (generic_expr.Expr a
                (Eaction
                  (Paction polarity.Pos
                    (Action loc ann
                      (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                        (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))),
              ρ, σ)

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.memop_eval :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {mop : memop}
  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v1 v2 : value} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 = some v1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some v2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop [pe1, pe2]), ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1), Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)]), ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.memop_op_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {mop : memop}
  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop [pe1, pe2]), ρ, σ) out →
      ∃ v1 v2,
        CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 = some v1 ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some v2 ∧
            out =
              (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1), Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)]), ρ,
                σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.memop_ptreq :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {b : Bool} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv2)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ = some (b, σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop PtrEq [pe1, pe2]), ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.memop_ptreq_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv2)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop PtrEq [pe1, pe2]), ρ, σ) out →
        ∃ b σ',
          CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ = some (b, σ') ∧
            out =
              (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)))), ρ,
                σ')
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.pure_eval :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe), ρ, σ)
        (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v))), ρ, σ)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.pure_inv :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
