def CerberusHeapLang.byteOwn :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] → Int → Iris.DFrac → CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] → Int → Iris.DFrac → List CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_append :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] (a : Int)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs₁ bs₂ : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq (bs₁ ++ bs₂) ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq bs₁ ∗ CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn (a + ↑bs₁.length) dq bs₂
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_cons :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] (a : Int)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (b : CerbMem.AbsByte) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq (b :: bs) =
    iprop(CerberusHeapLang.byteOwn a dq b ∗ CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn (a + 1) dq bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_get :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte) (a : Int) (dq : Iris.DFrac)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq bs ⊢
    ⌜∀ (j : Nat), j < bs.length → Iris.Std.get? mb (a + ↑j) = bs[j]?⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_nil :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] (a : Int)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac), CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq [] = iprop(emp)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_read :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (a : Int) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
      CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq bs ⊢
        ⌜CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a bs.length = bs⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn_update :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte) (a : Int) (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  bs'.length = bs.length →
    ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a (Iris.DFrac.own 1) bs ==∗
        CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp (CerberusHeapLang.insertRange mb a bs') ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a (Iris.DFrac.own 1) bs'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesToInt_go_cons :
∀ (p : CerbMem.Provenance) (c : Option Int) (v : UInt8) (rest : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (i : Nat)
  (acc : Int),
  CerbMem.bytesToInt.go ({ prov := p, copyOffset := c, value := some v } :: rest) i acc =
    CerbMem.bytesToInt.go rest (i + 1) (acc + ↑v.toNat <<< (i * 8))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesToInt_go_nil :
∀ (i : Nat) (acc : Int), CerbMem.bytesToInt.go [] i acc = acc
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesToInt_of_all_some :
∀ (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (bs.any fun x => x.value.isNone) = false →
    CerbMem.bytesToInt bs false = some (CerbMem.bytesToInt.go bs 0 0)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bytesToInt_ptrImg_cell :
∀ (id a : Int),
  0 ≤ a →
    a < 2 ^ 64 →
      CerbMem.bytesToInt (CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)) false = some a

∀ (v : value),
  select_case subst_sym_expr v
      [(CerberusHeapLang.symPat [] CerberusHeapLang.caseXSym BTy_unit,
          CerberusHeapLang.caseBranch)] =
    some (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))

  (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF) (v : value)

  ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun w x => iprop(⌜w.val = v⌝)) (CerberusHeapLang.caseProg v) ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.case_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (v : value),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx CerberusHeapLang.caseLs fun w x =>
      iprop(⌜w.val = v⌝)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.case_certified :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (v : value)
  (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  2 + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.caseProg v))
            σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.caseProg v))
            σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v' : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.caseProg v))
                σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v' σ' →
            v' = v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.case_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (v : value),
  ⊢
    WP
      { e := CerberusHeapLang.caseProg v, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv,
        M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @
      Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
      ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜w.val = v⌝ }}
----

def CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      Int → Iris.DFrac → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn_cellCoh :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (i : Int) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i dq c ⊢
        ⌜CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ i c ∧ Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] (i : Int)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i dq c ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn i dq (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c) ∗
      CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn c.addr dq c.bytes ∗
        ⌜c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty ∧ CerberusHeapLang.decIndep c.addr c.ty c.bytes⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn_view :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] (i : Int)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i dq c ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView i c.addr c.ty 0 dq dq c.ty c.bytes ∗
      ⌜CerberusHeapLang.decIndep c.addr c.ty c.bytes⌝

theorem CerberusHeapLang.cell_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),

    ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
      Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
        ⌜∃ i a,
            pv = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i { addr := a, ty := ty, bytes := bs }⌝

    CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c1) (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c2)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.cellsOwn_extract :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF]
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (Q : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      (CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗
            [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ Q, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
        ⌜CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ Q⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cellsOwn_facts :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF]
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (Q : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      (CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗
            [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ Q, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
        ⌜∀ (i : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
            Iris.Std.get? Q i = some c →
              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ i c ∧
                Iris.Std.get? mm i = some (CerberusHeapLang.metaOf c)⌝

CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint CerberusHeapLang.cellY CerberusHeapLang.cellX

theorem CerberusHeapLang.cells_readout :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF]
  (post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop) (R : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap) (vv : value),
  ((∃ Q, ⌜post vv Q⌝ ∗ [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ Q, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ∗
      [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ R, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
    ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
      Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
        ⌜∃ Q,
            post vv Q ∧
              Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q R ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ' (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q R)⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cells_to_mC :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.xPtr ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
      CerberusHeapLang.yPtr ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ;
        CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes ⊢
    [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ CerberusHeapLang.mC, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c

theorem CerberusHeapLang.collect_saves_loop :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int),
  collect_saves (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n) =
    CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.counter_loop_certified :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  (sbty : core_base_type) (idx addr : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
            (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton idx
              { addr := addr, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs0 }) →
          ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            4 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                have prog :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr) n;
                have rs :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopRS loc ann ra mo bty xbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr);
                (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                    ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog
                              [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                        v = Vunit ∧
                          ∃ bs',
                            (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                              ∃ i a,
                                CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                                  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                    { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.counter_loop_certified_production :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order) (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type)
  (idx addr : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
            (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton idx
              { addr := addr, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs0 }) →
          ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            4 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                have prog :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr) n;
                have rs :=
                  initial_core_run_state
                    (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile prog));
                (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                    ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                        v = Vunit ∧
                          ∃ bs',
                            (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                              ∃ i a,
                                CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                                  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                    { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.cursorHeap_update :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor} {c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor}
  (c' : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.cursorInterp mk ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn c ==∗
      CerberusHeapLang.cursorInterp (Iris.Std.insert mk 0 c') ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn c'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.cursorHeap_valid :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor}
  {c : CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.cursorInterp mk ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn c ==∗
      ⌜Iris.Std.get? mk 0 = some c⌝
----

def CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep :
Nat → core_run_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → core_step2 → CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_create_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {pref : prefix0}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {reqAddr : Option Int}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.PointerValue → thread_state} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty reqAddr none) σ =
      some (pv, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (CreateRequest2 pref align ty reqAddr none k))) =
      CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (k aid pv) σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_create_refusal :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {pref : prefix0}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {reqAddr : Option Int}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.PointerValue → thread_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty reqAddr none) σ = none →
    (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (CreateRequest2 pref align ty reqAddr none k)))).isRefusal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_load_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → CerbMem.MemValue → thread_state}
  {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw (stExceptUndef_return (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv k))) =
      CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (k aid fp mval) σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_load_refusal :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → CerbMem.MemValue → thread_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = none →
    (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv k)))).isRefusal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_memop_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : value → thread_state}
  {b : Bool},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ = some (b, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_memop_request2 loc PtrEq [Vobject (OVpointer pv1), Vobject (OVpointer pv2)] tid uw

      CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (k (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)) σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_store_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}

----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_store_refusal :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}

  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = none →

        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv k)))).isRefusal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.diverge_total_unprovable :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (Ls :
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGF)
  (Ψ :
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGF)
  (k : Nat),
  (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGF],
      ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT
            (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx CerberusHeapLang.dgProcSym (CerberusHeapLang.dgRS ra)) Ls Ψ ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt
            (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx CerberusHeapLang.dgProcSym (CerberusHeapLang.dgRS ra)) Ls k Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.dgBody ra) [fmapEmpty]) →
    False
----
def CerberusHeapLang.drive :
(Nat → Nat) → Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult
----
def CerberusHeapLang.driveJ :
core_run_state →
  (Nat → Nat) → Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_done :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) (v : value)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids (n + 1)
      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)
      σ =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_scrutinee :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e ρ)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs e ρ σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_step :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) {e e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
      {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.Step (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (e, ev0 :: evs, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids (n + 1) (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e (ev0 :: evs))
                σ =
              CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n
                (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e' ρ') σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_succ_eq :
∀ (rs : core_run_state) (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) (th : thread_state) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids (n + 1) th σ =
    match
      List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) rs σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) with
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] =>
      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
    | x => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_value_pure :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop)
  (aids : Nat → Nat) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  (∃ w, w.val = v ∧ φp w σ) →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
        (CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
          (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
            ρ)
          σ)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.driveU :
CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
  (Nat → Nat) → Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveU_procCtx :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat) (th : thread_state)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveU (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) aids n th σ =
    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n th σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveU_succ :
∀ (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) (th : thread_state)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids (n + 1) th σ =
    match CerberusHeapLang.stepOutcomes M (aids 0) th σ with
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] =>
      CerberusHeapLang.driveU M (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
    | x => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveU_value_pure :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.SeqWF →
    ∀ (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) (aids : Nat → Nat) (v : value)
      (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      (∃ w, w.val = v ∧ φp w σ) →
        ∀ (n : Nat),
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
            (CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids n
              (M.thread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ) σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_after_setup :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String) (dstD : driver_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne (driver2_lemFuel lemDefaultFuel fmapEmpty false)
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodEntryState e fs) =
      (NDactive (), dstD) →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
        (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
      (NDactive (finalize fmapEmpty "drive (without concur)" dstD), dstD)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_classifyU :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.SeqWF →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      ∀ (n₀ : Nat),
        (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
              CerberusHeapLang.esize cont + n₀ ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
          ∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
            (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
            (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
            (∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, M := M }, σ₀) (r, σ) →
                  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (r, σ)) →
              (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                  CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, M := M }, σ₀)
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w, σ) →
                    φp w σ) →
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  n ≤ n₀ →
                    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
                      (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, M := M }, σ₀)
                          ({ e := e, ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := M }, σ) →
                        CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
                          CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
                              (CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids n (M.thread e (ev0 :: evs)) σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_done :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v : value},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_next :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th th' : thread_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ =
      CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_scrutinee :
∀ (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_scrutinee_env :
∀ (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.envThread e ρ)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e ρ σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_done :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v : value},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_error :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {s : String},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.error s] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_killed :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {r : kill_reason mem_error},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_next :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th th' : thread_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ =
      CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_off :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.offFragment] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_succ_eq :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) (th : thread_state) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ =
    match
      List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) with
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] =>
      CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
    | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
    | x => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driver2_done :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (dst dstF : driver_state)
  (th thF : thread_state) (v : value),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemDefaultFuel tds fmapEmpty [0])
          dst =
        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] fmapEmpty), dstF) →
      dstF.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, thF)] →
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (driver2_lemFuel fl.succ tds false) dst =
          (NDactive (),
            { core_file := dstF.core_file, core_extern := dstF.core_extern,
              core_state0 :=
                have __src := dstF.core_state0;
                {
                  thread_states :=
                    [(0, none,
                        { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := thF.errno,
                          env := thF.env, current_proc_opt := thF.current_proc_opt,
                          exec_loc := thF.exec_loc, current_loc := thF.current_loc })],
                  io := __src.io },
              core_run_state0 := dstF.core_run_state0, layout_state := dstF.layout_state,
              concurrency_state := dstF.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dstF.fs_state0,
              trace := dstF.trace, symbolic_assoc := dstF.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dstF.blocked,
              dr_step_counter := dstF.dr_step_counter })

  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome

def CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP :
sym →
  Nat →
    core_run_state →
      CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
        CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP_done :
∀ (p : sym) (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs
      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ σ =
    [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP_eq :
∀ (p : sym) (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs e ρ σ =
    List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ) (CerberusHeapLang.engineStepsP p e ρ σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP_remove_annot :
∀ (p : sym) (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state) (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs
      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ρ σ =
    [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
          ρ)
        σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes_eq :
∀ (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e ρ σ =
    List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
      (CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ)

∀ (p : sym) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineStepsP p e ρ σ =
    step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
      (none, CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e ρ)

CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List core_step2

theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
              (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)))
                (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_pure :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq" TSK_Misc (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx e2) (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_done :
∀ (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                (CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                  (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b))) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
        (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
          ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
            CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
              [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                      CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                        (apply_ctx ctx
                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                            (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                              (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))))
                        ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_error2
                ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                  ("the value of a store(".append
                    ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                      (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                        (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_adequacyJ :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap}
  {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      ∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev00 : Fmap sym value) (evs0 : List (Fmap sym value))
        (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        CerberusHeapLang.Frag e₀ →
          CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
            ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
              (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
                  ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
                    WP { e := e₀, ρ := ev00 :: evs0, M := CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs } @
                      Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                      ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }}
                        ) →
                ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type))
                        (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
                          CerberusHeapLang.esize cont + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
                      (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                          CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                              (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                              (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                          ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ =
                                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                              ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_adequacyU :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.SeqWF →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      ∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev00 : Fmap sym value) (evs0 : List (Fmap sym value))
        (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        CerberusHeapLang.Frag e₀ →
          CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
            ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
              (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
                  ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
                    WP { e := e₀, ρ := ev00 :: evs0, M := M } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                      ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }}
                        ) →
                ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type))
                        (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
                          CerberusHeapLang.esize cont + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
                      (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                          CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids n (M.thread e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids n (M.thread e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                          ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                            CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids n (M.thread e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ =
                                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                              ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_complete :
∀ (aid : Nat) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∃ o,
        CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e (ev0 :: evs) σ = [o] ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e (ev0 :: evs) σ o
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_complete_caseU :
∀ (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (aid : Nat) {b : List annot} {cval : value}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats) ≤
      lemDefaultFuel →
    ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      ∃ o,
        CerberusHeapLang.outcomesU M aid (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats)
              ρ σ =
            [o] ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatchU M
            (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr b () (PEval cval)) pats) ρ σ o
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_complete_storeU :
∀ (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (aid : Nat) {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
  {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      ∃ o,
        CerberusHeapLang.outcomesU M aid (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ρ σ =
            [o] ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatchU M (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ρ σ o
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_step_matchU :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} (aid : Nat) {e e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ev0 :: evs, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.outcomesU M aid e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (M.thread e' ρ') σ']
----
def CerberusHeapLang.envAdd :
sym → value → Fmap sym value → Fmap sym value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.envAdd_lookup :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (cmp' : sym → sym → LemOrdering) (l k : sym) (v : value),
      fmapLookupBy cmp' l (CerberusHeapLang.envAdd k v f) =
        if CerberusHeapLang.symOrd l k = Ordering.eq then some v else fmapLookupBy cmp' l f
----
def CerberusHeapLang.envThread :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → thread_state
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.envThread_arena :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e ρ).arena = e
----

  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) (CerbMem.nullPtrval t)) σ =
    some (false, σ)

  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default (CerbMem.nullPtrval t) (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)) σ =

theorem CerberusHeapLang.eqPtrval_null_null :

  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
      (CerbMem.eqPtrval default (CerbMem.nullPtrval t1) (CerbMem.nullPtrval t2)) σ =

      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.esize :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.esize x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eif a_1 e2 e3) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e3)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esave a_1 a_2 body) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize body
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase a_1 pats) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esizeAlts pats
    | x => 1
----
def CerberusHeapLang.esizeAlts :
List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_action :
∀ {a : List annot} {p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_annot :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_memop :
∀ {a : List annot} {mop : memop} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop pes)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_pure :
∀ {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pos :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr), 1 ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pure :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : pexpr}, CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_sseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_wseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.eval1_bridge :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {th : thread_state} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe = some v →
    CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerbMem.MemState)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (rs : core_run_state),
        stExceptUndef_bind (E.eval_pexpr20 tds th ext σ file pe)
            (fun x =>
              match (motive :=
                pexpr ⊕ value → core_run_state → exceptM (t0 pexpr × core_run_state) core_run_cause)
                x with
              | Sum.inl pe' => stExceptUndef_return pe'
              | Sum.inr cval => stExceptUndef_return (mk_value_pe cval))
            rs =
          Result (Defined (mk_value_pe v), rs)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift :
ctype → value → value → Option value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift_long_one :
∀ (id a : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift CerberusHeapLang.longTy
      (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal 1) =
    some (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + 8))))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift_ptr_one :
∀ (id a : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift CerberusHeapLang.intTy
      (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal 1) =
    some (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + 4))))
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop :
binop → value → value → Option value
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr :
Fmap sym sym → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → generic_pexpr Unit sym → Option value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr.eq_def :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (x : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ x =
    match x with
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v) => some v
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x) => lookup_env (CerberusHeapLang.resolveExtern ext x) ρ
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2) => do
      let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe1
      let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe2
      CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop op v1 v2
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) => do
      let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe1
      let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe2
      CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift ty v1 v2
    | x => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr.induct :
∀ (motive : generic_pexpr Unit sym → Prop),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (v : value), motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v))) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (x : sym), motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x))) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
          motive pe1 → motive pe2 → motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2))) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
            (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
            motive pe1 → motive pe2 → motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2))) →
          (∀ (t : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
              (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (v : value), t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v) → False) →
                (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (x : sym), t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x) → False) →
                  (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                      t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2) → False) →
                    (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
                        (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                        t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) → False) →
                      motive t) →
            ∀ (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym), motive a
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_array_shift :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (ty : ctype)
  (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) = do
    let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe1
    let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe2
    CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift ty v1 v2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_none_of_shape :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (v : value), pe = Pexpr a u (PEval v) → False) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (x : sym), pe = Pexpr a u (PEsym x) → False) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
          pe = Pexpr a u (PEop op pe1 pe2) → False) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
            (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe = Pexpr a u (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) → False) →
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_op :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (op : binop)
  (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) = do
    let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe1
    let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe2
    CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop op v1 v2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_peStrip :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ (CerberusHeapLang.peStrip pe) =
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_shape :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe = some v → CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_sym :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (x : sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ (Pexpr a () (PEsym x)) =
    lookup_env (CerberusHeapLang.resolveExtern ext x) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_sym_empty :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (x : sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr fmapEmpty ρ (Pexpr a () (PEsym x)) = lookup_env x ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_val :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (v : value),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) = some v
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs :
Fmap sym sym → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → List (generic_pexpr Unit sym) → Option (List value)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs_cons :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
  (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ext ρ (pe :: pes) = do
    let v ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe
    let vs ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ext ρ pes
    pure (v :: vs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs_nil :
∀ (ext : Fmap sym sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ext ρ [] = some []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibit :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
    (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo)
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_prod :
∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
  ∃ dres dst',
    CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) args)
          (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) fs) =
        [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
      dres.dres_core_value = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧
        CerbMem.readBytesFrom dst'.layout_state CerberusHeapLang.pxAddr 4 =
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progA CerberusHeapLang.mA fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mA7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
      CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ₁
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999999 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = Vunit ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 =
                  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd ∧
                CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.bytesY
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progB
    (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union CerberusHeapLang.mA CerberusHeapLang.mF) fun v Q =>
    ∃ Q₀,
      (v = Vunit ∧ Q₀ = CerberusHeapLang.mA7) ∧
        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF ∧
          Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progC CerberusHeapLang.mB fun x Q =>
    Q = CerberusHeapLang.mC
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_triple :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation)
  (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy)
    (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_args_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs fmapEmpty
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          [CerberusHeapLang.fibIncPe, CerberusHeapLang.fibBPe, CerberusHeapLang.fibABPe] =
        some
          [CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (i + 1), CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b, CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (a + b)]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (p : sym)
  (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) →
    ∀ (i : Int) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
        0 ≤ i →
          i ≤ n →
            ⊢
              CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.fibLs n)
                (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n)
                (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i)
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec i.toNat))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (i.toNat + 1))) f ::
                  rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_body_wpt :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (p : sym)
  (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) →
    ∀ (i : Int) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
        0 ≤ i →
          i ≤ n →
            ⊢
              CerberusHeapLang.wpt (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.fibLsT n)
                (2 * (n - i).toNat + 3) (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n)
                (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i)
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec i.toNat))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (i.toNat + 1))) f ::
                  rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_certified :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors}
  [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (sbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
      3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        2 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          have prog := CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty;
          have rs := CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty;
          (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
              CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
              ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                      σ₀ =
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                  v = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_certified_total :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty sbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (aids : Nat → Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ (CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty) aids (2 * n.toNat + 4)
          (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym
            (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty) [fmapEmpty])
          σ₀ =
        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done
          (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)) σ₀
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_exit_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr fmapEmpty
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          CerberusHeapLang.fibExitPe =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_guard_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)) (n : Int),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr fmapEmpty
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibGuard n) =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue (decide (i < n)))

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_labeledAt_production :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (sbty ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt
    (initial_core_run_state
      (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty))))

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_terminates :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty sbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      Relation.StronglyNormalizing Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep
        ([{ e := CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty, ρ := [fmapEmpty],
              M :=
                CerberusHeapLang.procCtx CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym
                  (CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty) }],
          σ₀)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (p : sym)
  (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) →
    0 ≤ n →
      ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
        ⊢
          WP
            { e := CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty, ρ := [fmapEmpty],
              M := CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs } @
            Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
            ⊤ {{
              w,
                ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                  Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                    ⌜w.val = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)⌝ }}
----

  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (p : sym)

            (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty)
            [fmapEmpty]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_wpt :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (p : sym)

  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) →

theorem CerberusHeapLang.finalize_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (s : String) (dst : driver_state)
  (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    th.arena = mk_value_e v →
      finalize tds s dst =
        { dres_blocked := dst.blocked, dres_concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
          dres_driver_steps := dst.dr_step_counter, dres_core_value := v,
          dres_stdout := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stdout),
          dres_stderr := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stderr) }

  some CerberusHeapLang.fiveMval

CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt CerberusHeapLang.intTy CerberusHeapLang.fiveMval
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fmapLookupBy_addBy_empty :
∀ {β : Type} (cmpL cmpL' : sym → sym → LemOrdering) (k : sym) (v : β) (l : sym),
  fmapLookupBy cmpL' l (fmapAddBy cmpL k v fmapEmpty) =
    if lemCmpToOrd cmpL l k = Ordering.eq then some v else none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.foldM_args_bridge :
∀ {th : thread_state} {tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation} {ext : Fmap sym sym}
  (f :
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack →
      (sym × core_base_type) × generic_pexpr Unit sym →
        core_run_state → exceptM (t0 CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × core_run_state) core_run_cause),
  (∀ (acc : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (s : sym) (bTy : core_base_type)
      (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (rs' : core_run_state),
      f acc ((s, bTy), pe) rs' =
        stExceptUndef_bind (full_eval_pexpr tds th ext σ file pe)
          (fun cval => stExceptUndef_return (update_env (mk_sym_pat s bTy) cval acc)) rs') →
    ∀ (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)) (vs : List value)
      (acc : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (rs : core_run_state),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ext th.env pes = some vs →
        (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
          stExceptUndef_foldM f acc (params.zip pes) rs =
            Result (Defined (CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs acc), rs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.foldl_update_env_cons :
∀ {α : Type} (f : α → pattern) (g : α → value) (xs : List α) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  ∃ ev0', List.foldl (fun acc a => update_env (f a) (g a) acc) (ev0 :: evs) xs = ev0' :: evs
----

def CerberusHeapLang.freshBase :
Int → Int → Nat → Int
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.freshBase_add_le :
∀ (la alignN : Int) (ty : ctype),
  0 < CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty →
    CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) ≠ 0 →
      CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) + ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) ≤ la
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.freshBase_pos :
∀ (la alignN : Int) (ty : ctype),
  CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) ≠ 0 →
    0 < CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.full_eval_bridge :
∀ {b : Type} {ext : Fmap sym sym} {th : thread_state} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe = some v →
    CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerbMem.MemState)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation),
        full_eval_pexpr tds th ext σ file pe = stExceptUndef_return v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.genHeap_valid_big :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} {H : Type → Type} {L : Type u_1} {V : Type}
  [inst : Iris.Std.LawfulFiniteMap H L] [G : Iris.genHeapGS L V GF H] {σ m0 : H V}
  {dq : Iris.DFrac}, (Iris.genHeapInterp σ ∗ [∗map] l ↦ v ∈ m0, l ↦{dq} v) ⊢ ⌜m0 ⊆ σ⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.get?_union' :
∀ (m₁ m₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (k : Int),
  Iris.Std.get? (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union m₁ m₂) k =
    (Iris.Std.get? m₁ k).orElse fun x => Iris.Std.get? m₂ k

def CerberusHeapLang.insertRange :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte →
  Int → List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.insertRange.eq_def :
∀ (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte) (a : Int) (x : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.insertRange m a x =
    match x with
    | [] => m
    | b :: bs => CerberusHeapLang.insertRange (Iris.Std.insert m a b) (a + 1) bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.insertRange_get? :
∀ (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (k : Int),
  Iris.Std.get? (CerberusHeapLang.insertRange m a bs) k =
    if a ≤ k ∧ k < a + ↑bs.length then bs[(k - a).toNat]? else Iris.Std.get? m k

    CerbMem.intToBytes n sz =
