def CerberusHeapLang.isList :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] → CerbMem.PointerValue → List Int → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_cons :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (p : CerbMem.PointerValue) (v : Int) (vs : List Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.isList p (v :: vs) =
    iprop(∃ id aN q bs,
        ⌜p = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id aN ∧
              0 < aN ∧
                aN < 2 ^ 64 ∧
                  bs.length = 16 ∧
                    CerberusHeapLang.nodeValDec bs v ∧ CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec bs q⌝ ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
              { addr := aN, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
            CerberusHeapLang.isList q vs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_cons_intro :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id aN : Int) (q : CerbMem.PointerValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (v : Int) (vs : List Int),
  0 < aN →
    aN < 2 ^ 64 →
      bs.length = 16 →
        CerberusHeapLang.nodeValDec bs v →
          CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec bs q →
            CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                  { addr := aN, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
                CerberusHeapLang.isList q vs ⊢
              CerberusHeapLang.isList (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id aN) (v :: vs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_nil :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (p : CerbMem.PointerValue), CerberusHeapLang.isList p [] = iprop(⌜p = CerberusHeapLang.nullNode⌝)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_nil_intro :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF],
  ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.isList CerberusHeapLang.nullNode []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell}
  {mb : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte}
  {mk : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.AllocCursor},
  CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk →
    ∀ (ws : List Int) (p : CerbMem.PointerValue),
      CerberusHeapLang.isList p ws ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗ CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ⊢
        ⌜CerberusHeapLang.ChainAt σ p ws⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.isList_shape :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (p : CerbMem.PointerValue) (ws : List Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.isList p ws ⊢
    ⌜p = CerberusHeapLang.nullNode ∨
          ∃ id aN, p = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id aN ∧ 0 < aN ∧ aN < 2 ^ 64⌝ ∗
      CerberusHeapLang.isList p ws
----

def CerberusHeapLang.ivVal :

----
def CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Option (sym × List (generic_pexpr Unit sym))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Erun a_1 l pes) => some (l, pes)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 a_2) => CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq a_1 e1 a_2) => CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) =>
      if CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true then none else CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
    | x => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_action :
∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_annot :
∀ (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation) (b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) =
    if CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true then none else CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_annot_of_not_root :
∀ {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_annot_of_root :
∀ {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_memop :
∀ (a : List annot) (mop : memop) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop pes)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_ofVal :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal), CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_pure :
∀ (a : List annot) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_run :
∀ (a : List annot) (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Erun ra l pes)) = some (l, pes)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_sseq :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_wseq :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1 e2)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.language_toVal_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt), Iris.ProgramLogic.toVal r = CerberusHeapLang.toValRt r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lemNatBeq_iff :
∀ (n1 n2 : Nat), (n1 == n2) = true ↔ n1 = n2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.list_reverse_certified :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors}
  [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (sbty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int)
  (head : CerbMem.PointerValue) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.SeedChain m₀ head xs →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
          ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            6 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              5 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                have prog :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head;
                have rs := CerberusHeapLang.lrRS loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty;
                (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                    ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog
                              [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                        ∃ p',
                          v = CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal p' ∧ CerberusHeapLang.ChainAt σ' p' xs.reverse
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.list_reverse_certified_total :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty sbty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (head : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.SeedChain m₀ head xs →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
          ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
            ∃ p' σ',
              CerberusHeapLang.driveJ (CerberusHeapLang.lrRS loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty)
                    aids (13 * xs.length + 7)
                    (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym
                      (CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head)
                      [fmapEmpty])
                    σ₀ =
                  CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal p') σ' ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.ChainAt σ' p' xs.reverse
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.list_reverse_demo :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors}
  [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (sbty : core_base_type),
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ CerberusHeapLang.demoM →
        ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
          6 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            5 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              have prog :=
                CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty
                  CerberusHeapLang.demoHead;
              have rs := CerberusHeapLang.lrRS loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty;
              (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                  ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                          (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                          σ₀ =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                      ∃ p',
                        v = CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal p' ∧ CerberusHeapLang.ChainAt σ' p' [3, 2, 1]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.list_reverse_terminates :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty sbty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (head : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.SeedChain m₀ head xs →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
        Relation.StronglyNormalizing Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep
          ([{ e := CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head,
                ρ := [fmapEmpty],
                M :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.procCtx CerberusHeapLang.lrProcSym
                    (CerberusHeapLang.lrRS loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) }],
            σ₀)

def CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation → ctype → CerbMem.PointerValue → memory_order → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loadM_at :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat) (vty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id { addr := a, ty := aty } →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
      CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ (a + ↑off) (CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty) = bs →
        CerbMem.reconstructValue σ.lastUsedUnionMembers σ.funptrmap (a + ↑off) vty bs = mv →
          CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV vty mv = false →
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
                (CerbMem.loadM loc vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off))) σ =
              some
                ((CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.R (a + ↑off)
                      ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty),
                    mv),
                  σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loadM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap c = false →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc c.ty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr)) σ =
        some
          ((CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.R c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c),
            σ)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c) →
    ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0)
        (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c) →
    ∀ (i : Int) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.XReady f →
        0 ≤ i →
          i ≤ n →
            ⌜i = n⌝ ∗ c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ∨
                ⌜i < n⌝ ∗
                  c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ⊢
              CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0)
                (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0) (CerberusHeapLang.loopBody loc ann ra mo bty c)
                (CerberusHeapLang.envAdd CerberusHeapLang.xSym (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) f :: rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_labeledAt_production :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt
    (initial_core_run_state
      (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n))))
    CerberusHeapLang.mainSym (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_readout_val :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0 w.w w.ρ ⊢
    ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
      Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
        ⌜w.val = Vunit ∧
            ∃ bs',
              (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                ∃ i a,
                  c = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                    CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                      { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_action :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst dst' : driver_state} {th : thread_state}
  {s : String} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {req : action_request2 thread_state},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_action_request2 s loc 0 false (stExceptUndef_return req)] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (action_request_sequential2 loc 0 dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply req)
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0,
              core_run_state0 :=
                have __src := dst.core_run_state0;
                { tid_supply := __src.tid_supply, aid_supply := dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply + 1,
                  excluded_supply := __src.excluded_supply, sym_supply := __src.sym_supply,
                  labeled := __src.labeled },
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter } =
          (NDactive (), dst') →
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
          CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0]) dst'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_done :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th : thread_state} {v : value},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) = [Step_done2 v] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc), dst)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_tau :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th th' : thread_state}
  {s : String},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_tau2 s TSK_Misc th'] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0])
          (have __src :=
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0, core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0,
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter + 1 };
          { core_file := __src.core_file, core_extern := __src.core_extern,
            core_state0 := update_thread_state 0 th' dst.core_state0,
            core_run_state0 := __src.core_run_state0, layout_state := __src.layout_state,
            concurrency_state := __src.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := __src.fs_state0,
            trace := __src.trace, symbolic_assoc := __src.symbolic_assoc, blocked := __src.blocked,
            dr_step_counter := __src.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c) →
    0 ≤ n →
      ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
        c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ⊢
          WP
            { e := CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n, ρ := [fmapEmpty],
              M := CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs } @
            Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
            ⊤ {{
              w,
                ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                  Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                    ⌜w.val = Vunit ∧
                        ∃ bs',
                          (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                            ∃ i a,
                              c = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                                CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                  { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c) →
    0 ≤ n →
      ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
        c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0)
            (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0)
            (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n) [fmapEmpty]
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.lrQ_inv :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) {l : sym} {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
  {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) l =
      some (params, cont) →
    params = [(CerberusHeapLang.lrPrevSym, pbty), (CerberusHeapLang.lrCurSym, cbty)] ∧
      cont = CerberusHeapLang.lrBody loc ann ra mo bbty nbty ubty
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lrQ_lookup :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty)
      CerberusHeapLang.lrLoopSym =
    some
      ([(CerberusHeapLang.lrPrevSym, pbty), (CerberusHeapLang.lrCurSym, cbty)],
        CerberusHeapLang.lrBody loc ann ra mo bbty nbty ubty)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.lr_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) →
    ∀ (revd rest' : List Int) (pPrev pCur : CerbMem.PointerValue) (f : Fmap sym value)
      (renv : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
        xs = revd.reverse ++ rest' →
          CerberusHeapLang.isList pPrev revd ∗ CerberusHeapLang.isList pCur rest' ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.lrLs xs)
              (CerberusHeapLang.lrPost xs) (CerberusHeapLang.lrBody loc ann ra mo bbty nbty ubty)
              (CerberusHeapLang.lrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal pPrev)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal pCur) f ::
                renv)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lr_body_wpt :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) →
    ∀ (revd rest' : List Int) (pPrev pCur : CerbMem.PointerValue) (f : Fmap sym value)
      (renv : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
        xs = revd.reverse ++ rest' →
          CerberusHeapLang.isList pPrev revd ∗ CerberusHeapLang.isList pCur rest' ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wpt (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.lrLsT xs)
              (CerberusHeapLang.lrCost rest'.length) (CerberusHeapLang.lrPost xs)
              (CerberusHeapLang.lrBody loc ann ra mo bbty nbty ubty)
              (CerberusHeapLang.lrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal pPrev)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal pCur) f ::
                renv)
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.lr_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type) (head : CerbMem.PointerValue),
      CerberusHeapLang.isList head xs ⊢
        WP
          { e := CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head,
            ρ := [fmapEmpty], M := CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs } @
          Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
          ⊤ {{
            w,
              ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                  ⌜∃ p',
                      w.val = CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal p' ∧
                        CerberusHeapLang.ChainAt σ' p' xs.reverse⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lr_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type) (head : CerbMem.PointerValue),
      CerberusHeapLang.isList head xs ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.lrLs xs)
          (CerberusHeapLang.lrPost xs)
          (CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head) [fmapEmpty]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lr_wpt :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty : core_base_type) (xs : List Int) (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p (CerberusHeapLang.lrQ loc ann ra mo pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty) →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type) (head : CerbMem.PointerValue),
      CerberusHeapLang.isList head xs ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wpt (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) (CerberusHeapLang.lrLsT xs)
          (CerberusHeapLang.lrCost xs.length + 1) (CerberusHeapLang.lrPost xs)
          (CerberusHeapLang.lrProg loc ann ra mo sbty pbty cbty bbty nbty ubty head) [fmapEmpty]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.mapM_eval1_bridge :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {th : thread_state} {tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation}
  (f :
    generic_pexpr Unit sym →
      core_run_state → exceptM (t0 (generic_pexpr Unit sym) × core_run_state) core_run_cause),
  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (rs' : core_run_state),
      f pe rs' =
        stExceptUndef_bind (E.eval_pexpr20 tds th ext σ file pe)
          (fun x =>
            match (motive :=
              pexpr ⊕ value → core_run_state → exceptM (t0 pexpr × core_run_state) core_run_cause)
              x with
            | Sum.inl pe' => stExceptUndef_return pe'
            | Sum.inr cval => stExceptUndef_return (mk_value_pe cval))
          rs') →
    ∀ {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v1 v2 : value},
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe1 = some v1 →
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe2 = some v2 →
            CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              ∀ (rs : core_run_state),
                stExceptUndef_mapM f [pe1, pe2] rs =
                  Result (Defined [mk_value_pe v1, mk_value_pe v2], rs)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals :
value → value → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
def CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex :
memop → List (generic_pexpr Unit sym) → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
def CerberusHeapLang.mergeInto :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal
----
def CerberusHeapLang.metaDisjoint :
CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.metaHeap_alloc :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} {id : Int}
  (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
  Iris.Std.get? mm id = none →
    ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ==∗
        CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp (Iris.Std.insert mm id mc) ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1) mc
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.metaHeap_valid :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {mm : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell} {id : Int} {dq : Iris.DFrac}
  {mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗ CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id dq mc ==∗
      ⌜Iris.Std.get? mm id = some mc⌝
----
def CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.metaOf :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell
----
def CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] → Int → Iris.DFrac → CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn_agree :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id : Int) (dq₁ dq₂ : Iris.DFrac) (mc₁ mc₂ : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id dq₁ mc₁ ∗ CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id dq₂ mc₂ ⊢ ⌜mc₁ = mc₂⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn_fractional :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id : Int) (mc : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell) (q₁ q₂ : Iris.Qp),
  CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own (q₁ + q₂)) mc ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own q₁) mc ∗
      CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own q₂) mc
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn_ne :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {i₁ i₂ : Int} {dq₂ : Iris.DFrac} {mc₁ mc₂ : CerberusHeapLang.MetaCell},
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn i₁ (Iris.DFrac.own 1) mc₁ -∗
      CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn i₂ dq₂ mc₂ -∗ ⌜i₁ ≠ i₂⌝
----
def CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec :
List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerbMem.PointerValue → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec_ptrImg_cell :
∀ (id a : Int),
  0 < a →
    a < 2 ^ 64 →
      ∀ (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
        List.take 8 (List.drop 8 bs) = CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) →
          CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec bs (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec_ptrImg_null :
∀ (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  List.take 8 (List.drop 8 bs) = CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg CerberusHeapLang.nullNode →
    CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec bs CerberusHeapLang.nullNode
----

CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy = 8

theorem CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy_dec_indep :
∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy bs =
    CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy bs

theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_bytes_cell :
∀ (id a : Int) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))).snd =
    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))).snd
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_bytes_null :
∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy CerberusHeapLang.nullNode)).snd =
    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy CerberusHeapLang.nullNode)).snd

theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_compat :

  CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
      (CerbMem.typeofMval (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pv)) =
    true

theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_encodes :
∀ (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy))
      (CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal pv) =
    some (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pv)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_fpm_cell :
∀ (id a : Int) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))).fst =
    fpm
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_fpm_null :
∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy CerberusHeapLang.nullNode)).fst =
    fpm
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_img_cell :
∀ (id a : Int),
  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
        (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))).snd =
    CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_ptr_img_null :
(CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
      (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy CerberusHeapLang.nullNode)).snd =
  CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg CerberusHeapLang.nullNode
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.node_store_kit :
∀ (pPrev : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  (pPrev = CerberusHeapLang.nullNode ∨
      ∃ id aN, pPrev = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id aN ∧ 0 < aN ∧ aN < 2 ^ 64) →
    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pPrev)).snd.length =
        8 ∧
      (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pPrev)).fst =
            fpm) ∧
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pPrev)).snd =
              (CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
                  (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pPrev)).snd) ∧
          ∀ (bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
            List.take 8 (List.drop 8 bs') =
                (CerbMem.memValueToBytes []
                    (CerbMem.pointerMval CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy pPrev)).snd →
              CerberusHeapLang.nodeNextDec bs' pPrev
----

def CerberusHeapLang.peDepth :
generic_pexpr Unit sym → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth.eq_def :
∀ (x : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth x =
    match x with
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEop a_2 pe1 pe2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 a_2 pe2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
    | x => 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_array_shift :
∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_op :
∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_peStrip :
∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (CerberusHeapLang.peStrip pe) = CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_pos :
∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), 1 ≤ CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_sym_le :
∀ (pb : List annot) (x : sym), CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)) ≤ lemDefaultFuel
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_val_le :
∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) ≤ lemDefaultFuel
----
def CerberusHeapLang.peStrip :
generic_pexpr Unit sym → generic_pexpr Unit sym
----
def CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerbMem.PointerValue → Iris.DFrac → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell_cellOwn_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊣⊢
    ∃ id a,
      ⌜pv = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a⌝ ∗
        CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := ty, bytes := bs }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊣⊢
    ∃ i a,
      ⌜pv = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a⌝ ∗
        CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn i dq { addr := a, ty := ty } ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn a dq bs ∗
            ⌜bs.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty ∧ CerberusHeapLang.decIndep a ty bs⌝
----
def CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      Int →
        Int → ctype → Nat → Iris.DFrac → Iris.DFrac → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat) (dqm dqb : Iris.DFrac) (vty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm dqb vty bs ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.metaOwn id dqm { addr := a, ty := aty } ∗
      ⌜off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty ∧
            bs.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty⌝ ∗
        CerberusHeapLang.bytesOwn (a + ↑off) dqb bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView_join :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat) (q₁ q₂ : Iris.Qp) (dqb : Iris.DFrac)
  (vty vty₁ vty₂ : ctype) (bs₁ bs₂ : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₁ + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₂ →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
      CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off (Iris.DFrac.own q₁) dqb vty₁ bs₁ ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty (off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₁)
            (Iris.DFrac.own q₂) dqb vty₂ bs₂ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off (Iris.DFrac.own (q₁ + q₂)) dqb vty (bs₁ ++ bs₂)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView_split :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat) (q₁ q₂ : Iris.Qp) (dqb : Iris.DFrac)
  (vty vty₁ vty₂ : ctype) (bs₁ bs₂ : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₁ + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₂ →
    bs₁.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₁ →
      CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off (Iris.DFrac.own (q₁ + q₂)) dqb vty (bs₁ ++ bs₂) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off (Iris.DFrac.own q₁) dqb vty₁ bs₁ ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty (off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty₁)
            (Iris.DFrac.own q₂) dqb vty₂ bs₂
----
def CerberusHeapLang.pot :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.pot x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e1) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e1) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.pot b
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eif a_1 e2 e3) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e3)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esave a_1 a_2 body) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.pot body
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase a_1 pats) => 2 * (1 + CerberusHeapLang.esizeAlts pats)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Epure (Pexpr a_1 a_2 (PEval a_3))) => 1
    | x => 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_action :
∀ {a : List annot} {p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_annot :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = 1 + CerberusHeapLang.pot b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_case :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats)) =
    2 * (1 + CerberusHeapLang.esizeAlts pats)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_if :
∀ {a : List annot} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e3)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_memop :
∀ {a : List annot} {mop : memop} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop pes)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_ofVal_annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_ofVal_pure :
∀ {v : value}, CerberusHeapLang.pot (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_pure_sym :
∀ {a pb : List annot} {x : sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)))) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_pure_val :
∀ {a pb : List annot} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure (Pexpr pb () (PEval v)))) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_run :
∀ {a : List annot} {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Erun ra l pes)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_save :
∀ {a : List annot} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Esave sb ps body)) = 1 + CerberusHeapLang.pot body
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_sseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e1) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pot_wseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.pot (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.pot e1) (CerberusHeapLang.pot e2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prepare_exit_single :
∀ (cs : core_state) (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    prepare_exit cs v =
      {
        thread_states :=
          [(0, none,
              { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                current_loc := th.current_loc })],
        io := cs.io }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.primStep_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (obs : List Empty)
  (r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (efs : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep (r, σ) obs (r', σ', efs) ↔
    CerberusHeapLang.Step r.M (r.e, r.ρ, σ) (r'.e, r'.ρ, σ') ∧ r'.M = r.M ∧ efs = []
----

    (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs).labels = Q

theorem CerberusHeapLang.process_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (cont : Bool → ndM Unit step_kind driver_error (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
  (v : value) (dst : driver_state) (th : thread_state),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (process_core_step2 tds false cont (Step_done2 v)) dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 :=
            have __src := dst.core_state0;
            {
              thread_states :=
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno,
                      env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                      exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc })],
              io := __src.io },
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := dst.layout_state,
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dst.blocked,
          dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_pre :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (2 + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProd)
      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 2)) n
      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC) CerberusHeapLang.σcP
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC)
      CerberusHeapLang.σcP =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ1P
----

  fmapLookupBy (fun s1 s2 => Lem_Basic_classes.ordCompare s1 s2) CerberusHeapLang.mainSym
      (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e).funs =

def CerberusHeapLang.prodThread :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → thread_state
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_loop_done :
∀ (th₀ : thread_state),
  th₀.stack0 = Stack_empty →
    ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
      th₀.env = ev0 :: evs →
        ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (n fl : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
          (dst : driver_state) (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
          CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e →
            dst.core_state0.thread_states =
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := e, stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th₀.current_loc })] →
              CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                n + 2 ≤ fl →
                  (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                          dst.layout_state =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                    ∃ rs' tr ctr,
                      CerberusHeapLang.runOne
                          (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl fmapEmpty acc [0]) dst =
                        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc),
                          { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
                            core_state0 :=
                              have __src := dst.core_state0;
                              {
                                thread_states :=
                                  [(0, none,
                                      {
                                        arena :=
                                          CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v),
                                        stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                                        current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt,
                                        exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                                        current_loc := th₀.current_loc })],
                                io := __src.io },
                            core_run_state0 := rs', layout_state := σfin,
                            concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
                            trace := tr, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
                            blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := ctr })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_run_eq :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e →
    ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
      (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e) CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e + k + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
            ∃ dres dst',
              CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                  dres.dres_blocked = false ∧
                    dres.dres_stdout = "" ∧ dres.dres_stderr = "" ∧ dst'.layout_state = σfin
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.provenA :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF CerberusHeapLang.progA CerberusHeapLang.mA fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mA7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.provenAProd :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC CerberusHeapLang.mAP fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mAP7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.provenB :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF CerberusHeapLang.progB CerberusHeapLang.mA fun v Q =>
    v = Vunit ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mA7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.provenC :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF CerberusHeapLang.progC CerberusHeapLang.mB fun x Q =>
    Q = CerberusHeapLang.mC

∀ (p : CerbMem.Provenance), (p == p) = true

def CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg :
CerbMem.PointerValue → List CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg_cell :
∀ (id a : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) =
    List.map
      (fun x =>
        match x with
        | (v, i) => { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some ↑i, value := v })
      ((CerbMem.intToBytes a 8).zip (List.range (CerbMem.intToBytes a 8).length))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg_cell_explicit :
∀ (id a : Int),
  0 ≤ a →
    CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) =
      [{ prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 0,
          value := some ((a >>> (0 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 1,
          value := some ((a >>> (1 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 2,
          value := some ((a >>> (2 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 3,
          value := some ((a >>> (3 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 4,
          value := some ((a >>> (4 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 5,
          value := some ((a >>> (5 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 6,
          value := some ((a >>> (6 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 },
        { prov := CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id, copyOffset := some 7,
          value := some ((a >>> (7 * 8)).toNat % 256).toUInt8 }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg_cell_length :
∀ (id a : Int), (CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)).length = 8
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg_null_length :
(CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg CerberusHeapLang.nullNode).length = 8
----
def CerberusHeapLang.ptrVal :
CerbMem.PointerValue → value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptx_to_cells :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.xPtr ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ;
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd ⊢
    [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ CerberusHeapLang.mA7, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ptx_to_cells_P :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.pxPtr ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ;
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd ⊢
    [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ CerberusHeapLang.mAP7, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pull_bridge :
∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    ∀ (fuel : Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ fuel →
        ∀ (n : Nat), pull_constrained_lemFuel fuel n pe = CerberusHeapLang.peStrip pe

def CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap :
Int → List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap.eq_def :
∀ (a : Int) (x : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap a x =
    match x with
    | [] => ∅
    | b :: bs => Iris.Std.insert (CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap (a + 1) bs) a b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap_get? :
∀ (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (k : Int),
  Iris.Std.get? (CerberusHeapLang.rangeMap a bs) k =
    if a ≤ k ∧ k < a + ↑bs.length then bs[(k - a).toNat]? else none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_eq_map_byteAt :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (n : Nat),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a n =
    List.map (fun i => CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ (a + ↑i)) (List.range n)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_of_byteAt :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < bs.length), CerberusHeapLang.byteAt σ (a + ↑i) = bs[i]) →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a bs.length = bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_sub :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (m : Nat) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a m = bs →
    ∀ (off k : Nat),
      off + k ≤ m → CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ (a + ↑off) k = List.take k (List.drop off bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_writeBytesTo_disjoint :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (a' : Int) (n : Nat),
  a' + ↑n ≤ a ∨ a + ↑bs.length ≤ a' →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs) a' n = CerbMem.readBytesFrom st a' n
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_writeBytesTo_self :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs) a bs.length = bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_write_interior :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (off : Nat) (img : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (n : Nat)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a n = bs →
    off + img.length ≤ n →
      CerbMem.readBytesFrom (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ (a + ↑off) img) a n =
        CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs
----
def CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      (value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) →
        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.reconstruct_ptrImg_cell :
∀ (id a : Int),
  0 < a →
    a < 2 ^ 64 →
      ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
            (CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)) =
          CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.reconstruct_ptrImg_null :
∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
  CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
      (CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg CerberusHeapLang.nullNode) =
    CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy CerberusHeapLang.nullNode

theorem CerberusHeapLang.runND_active :

theorem CerberusHeapLang.runOne_bind_active :
∀ {a b cs err info st : Type} {m : ndM a info err cs st} {f : a → ndM b info err cs st} {s s' : st}
  {z : a},
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne m s = (NDactive z, s') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (nd_bind m f) s = CerberusHeapLang.runOne (f z) s'

  CerberusHeapLang.runOne m s = (NDkilled r, s') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (nd_bind m f) s = (NDkilled r, s')

theorem CerberusHeapLang.runOne_liftCore_run_aid :
∀ (dst : driver_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne (liftCore_run (runS fresh_action_id')) dst =
    (NDactive dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply,
      { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern, core_state0 := dst.core_state0,
        core_run_state0 :=
          have __src := dst.core_run_state0;
          { tid_supply := __src.tid_supply, aid_supply := dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply + 1,
            excluded_supply := __src.excluded_supply, sym_supply := __src.sym_supply,
            labeled := __src.labeled },
        layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
        fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
        blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.runOne_liftCore_run_return :
∀ {a : Type} (z : a) (dst : driver_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne (liftCore_run (stExceptUndef_return z)) dst = (NDactive z, dst)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.runOne_liftMem_active :
∀ {a : Type} {m : ndM a String mem_error (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) CerbMem.MemState}
  {dst : driver_state} {z : a} {σ' : CerbMem.MemState},
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne m dst.layout_state = (NDactive z, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (liftMem m) dst =
      (NDactive z,
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 := dst.core_state0, core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0,
          layout_state := σ', concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
          fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
          blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.runOne_of_applyMemM :
∀ {α : Type} {m : CerbMem.memM α} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {z : α},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM m σ = some (z, σ') → CerberusHeapLang.runOne m σ = (NDactive z, σ')

theorem CerberusHeapLang.semAProd :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC CerberusHeapLang.mAP fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mAP7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sem_triple_prod :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag e →
    ∀ (ec : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      (post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop),
      CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple ec P post →
        ∀ (R : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap),
          Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P R →
            ∀ (σc : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (j : Nat),
              (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (j + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + j)) n
                      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc) →
                CerberusHeapLang.Sat σc (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P R) →
                  ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
                    (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                      CerberusHeapLang.esize e + (j + k) + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                        CerberusHeapLang.esize ec + k ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
                            ∃ dres dst',
                              CerbND.runND
                                    (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                                  dst'.layout_state = σfin ∧
                                    ∃ Q,
                                      post v Q ∧
                                        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q R ∧
                                          CerberusHeapLang.Sat σfin (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_frame :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} (F : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P F →
        CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post →
          CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P F) fun v Q =>
            ∃ Q₀, post v Q₀ ∧ Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ F ∧ Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ F
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_triple_sound :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post → CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e P post
