theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_engine_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.Frag e₀ →
    CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
      ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
        (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
            ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
              WP { e := e₀, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @
                Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }} ) →
          ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                  ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                      ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_step_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m₀ →
    ∀ (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
      (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
          ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
            WP e @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
              ⊤ {{ v, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜φp v σ'⌝ }} ) →
        ∀ {t2 : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ2 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
          FromMathlib.Relation.ReflTransGen Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep ([e], σ)
              (t2, σ2) →
            (∀ (e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                e2 ∈ t2 → Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (e2, σ2)) ∧
              ∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (t2' : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                t2 = CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w :: t2' → φp w σ2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_wp_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {Φ Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP e @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢ (∀ v, Φ v -∗ Ψ v) -∗ WP e @ s ; E {{ Ψ }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes :
Nat → List CerbMem.AbsByte → List CerbMem.AbsByte → List CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_getElem? :
∀ (off : Nat) (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  off + img.length ≤ bs.length →
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs)[k]? =
        if k < off then bs[k]? else if k < off + img.length then img[k - off]? else bs[k]?
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_length :
∀ (off : Nat) (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  off + img.length ≤ bs.length → (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs).length = bs.length
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_next_slice :
∀ (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  img.length = 8 →
    bs.length = 16 → List.take 8 (List.drop 8 (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes 8 img bs)) = img
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_slice_above :
∀ (off : Nat) (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  off + img.length ≤ bs.length →
    ∀ (o' n : Nat),
      off + img.length ≤ o' →
        List.take n (List.drop o' (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs)) =
          List.take n (List.drop o' bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_slice_below :
∀ (off : Nat) (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  off + img.length ≤ bs.length →
    ∀ (o' n : Nat),
      o' + n ≤ off →
        List.take n (List.drop o' (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs)) =
          List.take n (List.drop o' bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_slice_self :
∀ (off : Nat) (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  off + img.length ≤ bs.length →
    List.take img.length (List.drop off (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off img bs)) = img
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes_value_slice :
∀ (img bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  img.length = 8 →
    bs.length = 16 →
      List.take 8 (List.drop 0 (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes 8 img bs)) =
        List.take 8 (List.drop 0 bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.splitBytesProv_ptrImg_cell_fst :
∀ (id a : Int),
  0 ≤ a →
    (CerbMem.splitBytesProv (CerberusHeapLang.ptrImg (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))).fst =
      CerbMem.Provenance.Prov_some id
----
def CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr :
core_base_type → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_annot_det :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty}
  {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := M },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧
      σ' = σ ∧ r' = { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := M } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_pure_det :
∀ {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty} {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := M },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧ σ' = σ ∧ r' = { e := e2, ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := M } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_bind_apply :
∀ {a b c d e : Type} (m : e → exceptM (t0 d × a) c) (f : d → a → exceptM (t0 b × a) c) (st : e),
  stExceptUndef_bind m f st =
    match m st with
    | Result (Defined z, st') => f z st'
    | Result (Undef loc1 ubs, st') => stExpect_return (undef loc1 ubs) st'
    | Result (Error loc1 str, st') => stExpect_return (error0 loc1 str) st'
    | exceptM.Exception err => fail0 err
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_foldM_cons :
∀ {a b c e : Type} (f : a → e → c → exceptM (t0 a × c) b) (acc : a) (x : e) (xs : List e),
  stExceptUndef_foldM f acc (x :: xs) =
    stExceptUndef_bind (f acc x) fun z => stExceptUndef_foldM f z xs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_return_apply :
∀ {a b c : Type} (z : a) (st : b), stExceptUndef_return z st = Result (Defined z, st)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExpect_bind_apply :
∀ {a b msg s : Type} (m : s → exceptM (a × s) msg) (f : a → s → exceptM (b × s) msg) (st : s),
  stExpect_bind m f st =
    match m st with
    | Result (a1, s') => f a1 s'
    | exceptM.Exception err => exceptM.Exception err
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExpect_bind_result :
∀ {a b msg s : Type} (m : s → exceptM (a × s) msg) (f : a → s → exceptM (b × s) msg) (st st' : s)
  (x : a), m st = Result (x, st') → stExpect_bind m f st = f x st'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.stateInert :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Bool
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInert.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.stateInert x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e1 && CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e2
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e1 && CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e2
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) => CerberusHeapLang.stateInert b
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eif a_1 e2 e3) =>
      CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e2 && CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e3
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esave a_1 a_2 body) => CerberusHeapLang.stateInert body
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction a_1) => false
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop a_1 a_2) => false
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase a_1 a_2) => false
    | x => true
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt =
    iprop(∃ mm mb mk,
        ⌜CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk⌝ ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗
            CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cursorInterp mk)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt ⊣⊢
    ∃ mm mb mk,
      ⌜CerberusHeapLang.CohG σ mm mb mk⌝ ∗
        CerberusHeapLang.metaInterp mm ∗
          CerberusHeapLang.byteInterp mb ∗ CerberusHeapLang.cursorInterp mk
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_if_false :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env g = some Vfalse →
              ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                    (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                  [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx e3, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                        exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }
                      σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_if_true :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env g = some Vtrue →
              ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                    (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                  [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx e2, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                        exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }
                      σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_load_eval :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
        CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
          CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
              (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
              (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
                  ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                    List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                        (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                          { arena := apply_ctx ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo),
                            stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                            current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                            current_loc := th.current_loc }
                          σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_memop_eval :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {mop : memop} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {v1 v2 : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [pe1, pe2]) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
              (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
              (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe1 = some v1 →
                  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe2 = some v2 →
                    ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                      List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                          (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                        [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                            {
                              arena :=
                                apply_ctx ctx
                                  (generic_expr.Expr []
                                    (Ememop mop [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1), Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)])),
                              stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                              current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                              current_loc := th.current_loc }
                            σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_pure_sym :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pb : List annot} {x : sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)) = some v →
            ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
              List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                  (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }
                    σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_run :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym}
  {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
        {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value},
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
          (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
              pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
            ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
              (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
              (parent : Option Nat) (p : sym) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                th.current_proc_opt = some p →
                  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ext th.env pes = some vs →
                    ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                      CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs (CerberusHeapLang.resolveExtern ext p) Q →
                        List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                            (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                          [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                              { arena := cont, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                                env := CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs th.env,
                                current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                                current_loc := th.current_loc }
                              σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_store_eval :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
        valueFromPexpr pe3 = none →
          CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
            CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe3 →
              CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe3 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                  ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
                    (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
                    (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
                    th.arena = e →
                      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
                        CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext th.env pe3 = some cv →
                          ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                            List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                                (step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th)) =
                              [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                                  {
                                    arena :=
                                      apply_ctx ctx
                                        (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann false ty pv cv mo),
                                    stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                                    current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                                    exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }
                                  σ]
----
def CerberusHeapLang.stepOutcomes :
CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
  Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepOutcomes_thread :
∀ (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.stepOutcomes M aid (M.thread e ρ) σ = CerberusHeapLang.outcomesU M aid e ρ σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
            th.env = ev0 :: evs →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----

                      env :=
                        update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov))

                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_case_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {a : List annot} {cval : value}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr a () (PEval cval)) pats) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = none →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_error2
                  ("Ecase, mismatched ==> ".append
                    (CerbPP.stringFromCore_expr
                      (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr a () (PEval cval)) pats)))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_case_value :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {a : List annot} {cval : value}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr a () (PEval cval)) pats) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_tau2 "Ecase" TSK_Misc
                  { arena := apply_ctx ctx e', stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                    env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                    current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_done :
∀ (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
  (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) →
    th.stack0 = Stack_empty → step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (none, th) = [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_memop :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {mop : memop} {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {v1 v2 : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [pe1, pe2]) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexpr pe1 = some v1 →
        valueFromPexpr pe2 = some v2 →
          ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
            (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
            (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
            th.arena = e →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_memop_request2 th.current_loc mop [v1, v2] tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                    fun cval =>
                    {
                      arena :=
                        apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval cval)))),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                  { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b)),
                    stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                    current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                    current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym)
  (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) →
    step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
      [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
          { arena := CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v), stack0 := th.stack0,
            errno := th.errno, env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
            exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_save :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {cvals : List value},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) = some cvals →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
              th.env = ev0 :: evs →
                step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                  [Step_tau2 "Esave" TSK_Misc
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx body, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs),
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
          memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
            ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
              (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                  [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                      (stExceptUndef_return
                        (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                          {
                            arena :=
                              apply_ctx ctx
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                    (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))),
                            stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                            current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                            current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
        memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
          ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
            (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
            th.arena = e →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_error2
                    ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                      ("the value of a store(".append
                        ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                          (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                            (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_wseq_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []

        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
            th.env = ev0 :: evs →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_tau2 "Ewseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_eval_bridge :
∀ {ext : Fmap sym sym} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ext ρ pe = some v →
      ∀ (fuel : Nat),
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ fuel →
          ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (n : Nat) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
            (cloc : Option CerbLocation.Loc) (mem : Option CerbMem.MemState)
            (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation),
            step_eval_pexpr_lemFuel fuel tds n loc cloc ext ρ mem file false pe =
              exception_undef_return (Pexpr [] () (PEval v))

def CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    ctype → CerbMem.PointerValue → value → memory_order → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.storeM_at :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat) (vty : ctype)
  (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.MetaCoh σ id { addr := a, ty := aty } →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
      CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible vty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
          (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
              (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
                (CerbMem.storeM loc vty false (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mv) σ =
              some
                (CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.W (a + ↑off)
                    ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty),
                  CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ (a + ↑off) (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.storeM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
          (CerbMem.storeM loc c.ty false (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr) mv) σ =
        some
          (CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.W c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
            CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.store_applies :
CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.xPtr
      CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval)
    CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
  some (CerberusHeapLang.fpS, CerberusHeapLang.σ₁)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.store_applies_P :
CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.pxPtr
      CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval)
    CerberusHeapLang.σcP =
  some (CerberusHeapLang.fpSP, CerberusHeapLang.σ1P)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.straightFrag_ofVal :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal), CerberusHeapLang.StraightFrag (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_gt :
∀ {d1 d2 : String}, ¬d1 < d2 → d1 ≠ d2 → Ord.compare d1 d2 = Ordering.gt
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_lt :
∀ {d1 d2 : String}, d1 < d2 → Ord.compare d1 d2 = Ordering.lt
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_self :
∀ (d : String), Ord.compare d d = Ordering.eq

def CerberusHeapLang.structTy :
ctype
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.structTy_decIndep :
∀ (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte), CerberusHeapLang.decIndep a CerberusHeapLang.structTy bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.structTy_dec_indep :
∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr CerberusHeapLang.structTy bs =
    CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr CerberusHeapLang.structTy bs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.structTy_nonatomic :
CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy CerberusHeapLang.structTy = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.structTy_size :
CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.structTy = 16
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.struct_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (id a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx (fun x x_1 x_2 => iprop(False))
      fun x x_1 =>
      CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
        { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy,
          bytes :=
            CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldY CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
              (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldX CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes bs) }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.struct_create_store_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (aprov : CerbMem.Provenance) (alignN : Int) (pref : prefix0)
  (mo : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (la nid : Int) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.structTy) ≠ 0 →
    CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn { lastAddr := la, nextId := nid } ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
        (fun x x_1 =>
          iprop(CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn nid (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                {
                  addr :=
                    CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN
                      (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.structTy),
                  ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy,
                  bytes :=
                    CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldX CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes
                      (List.replicate (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.structTy)
                        CerberusHeapLang.undefByte) } ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn
                {
                  lastAddr :=
                    CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN
                      (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.structTy),
                  nextId := nid + 1 }))
        (CerberusHeapLang.progCreateInit loc ann aprov alignN pref mo bty la nid) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.struct_update_certified :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (id a : Int)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
          (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton id
            { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy, bytes := bs }) →
        ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
          2 + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
                    (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.progS loc ann mo mo' bty id a))
                    σ₀ ≠
                  CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
              CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
                    (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.progS loc ann mo mo' bty id a))
                    σ₀ ≠
                  CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
                        (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread
                          (CerberusHeapLang.progS loc ann mo mo' bty id a))
                        σ₀ =
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                    CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' (a + ↑CerberusHeapLang.fieldX) 4 =
                        CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∧
                      CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' (a + ↑CerberusHeapLang.fieldY) 4 =
                        CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.struct_wp_readout :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo mo' : memory_order)
  (bty : core_base_type) (id a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
      { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy, bytes := bs } ⊢
    WP
      { e := CerberusHeapLang.progS loc ann mo mo' bty id a, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv,
        M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @
      Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
      ⊤ {{
        w,
          ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
            Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
              ⌜CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' id
                  { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy,
                    bytes :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldY CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
                        (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldX
                          CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes bs) }⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.struct_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (id a : Int)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
      { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy, bytes := bs } ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun x x_1 =>
        CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
          { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy,
            bytes :=
              CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldY CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
                (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldX CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes
                  bs) })
      (CerberusHeapLang.progS loc ann mo mo' bty id a) (ev0 :: evs)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK :
sym → sym → LemOrdering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK_eq :
∀ (d1 : String) (n1 : Nat) (sd1 : symbol_description) (d2 : String) (n2 : Nat)
  (sd2 : symbol_description),
  CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) =
    if
        (intLtb (CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2) 0 ||
            CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2 == 0 && natLtb n1 n2) =
          true then
      LemOrdering.LT
    else
      if symbolEquality (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) = true then LemOrdering.EQ
      else LemOrdering.GT
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symCmpL :
sym → sym → LemOrdering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symFrame_empty :
CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame fmapEmpty
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symKey :
sym → String × Nat
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symOrd :
sym → sym → Ordering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symOrd_eq_compareOn :
CerberusHeapLang.symOrd =
  compareLex (compareOn fun s => (CerberusHeapLang.symKey s).fst)
    (compareOn fun s => (CerberusHeapLang.symKey s).snd)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symPat :
List annot → sym → core_base_type → pattern
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symPat_inj :
∀ {pa pa' : List annot} {x x' : sym} {bty bty' : core_base_type},
  CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty = CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa' x' bty' →
    pa = pa' ∧ x = x' ∧ bty = bty'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symPat_ne_base :
∀ {pa' : List annot} {bty' : core_base_type} {pa : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type},
  Pattern pa' (CaseBase (none, bty')) = CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty → False
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symPat_ne_spec :
∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {pa' : List annot} {x' : sym}
  {bty' : core_base_type},
  CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty = CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa' x' bty' → False
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symbolEquality_eq :
∀ (d1 : String) (n1 : Nat) (sd1 : symbol_description) (d2 : String) (n2 : Nat)
  (sd2 : symbol_description),
  symbolEquality (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) =
    (CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2 == 0 && n1 == n2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.take_sum_succ :
∀ (vs : List Int) (i : Nat) (h : i < vs.length),
  (List.take (i + 1) vs).sum = (List.take i vs).sum + vs[i]
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_mk :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx),
  CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } =
    Option.map (fun w => { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M }) (CerberusHeapLang.toVal e)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_ofValRt :
∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal), CerberusHeapLang.toValRt (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt v) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_of_toVal :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = some w →
    CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = some { w := w, ρ := ρ, M := M }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_of_toVal_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none → CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_action_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_cases :
∀ (a : List annot) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  (a = [] ∧
      ∃ v,
        c = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) =
            some (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ∨
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_none :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds e)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_memop_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (mop : memop) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Ememop mop pes)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_ofVal :
∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal), CerberusHeapLang.toVal (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal v) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_pure_none :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  valueFromPexpr pe = none → CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_sseq_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_wseq_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq pat e1 e2)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.treeMap_get?_insert_empty :
∀ {α β : Type} (cmp : α → α → Ordering) (k : α) (v : β) (l : α),
  (Std.TreeMap.empty.insert k v).get? l = if cmp l k = Ordering.eq then some v else none
----
def CerberusHeapLang.triple :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      Iris.IProp GF → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF) → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_conseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P' P : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ Ψ' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (P' ⊢ P) →
    (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal), Ψ v ⊢ Ψ' v) →
      CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple P' e Ψ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P R : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple iprop(P ∗ R) e fun v => iprop(Ψ v ∗ R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P Q R : Iris.IProp GF} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1 →
    (CerberusHeapLang.triple P e1 fun x => Q) →
      (CerberusHeapLang.triple Q e2 fun x => R) →
        CerberusHeapLang.triple P (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty e1 e2) fun x => R
----
def CerberusHeapLang.undefByte :
CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_aux_spec :
∀ (pa pb : List annot) (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (ov : object_value) (m : Fmap sym value),
  update_env_aux (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) m =
    CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x (Vobject ov) m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_aux_sym :
∀ (x : sym) (b : core_base_type) (v : value) (m : Fmap sym value),
  update_env_aux (mk_sym_pat x b) v m = CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x v m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_cons :
∀ (pat : pattern) (v : value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  update_env pat v (ev0 :: evs) = update_env_aux pat v ev0 :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_spec :
∀ (pa pb : List annot) (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (ov : object_value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) (ev0 :: evs) =
    CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x (Vobject ov) ev0 :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_thread_state_single :
∀ (th th' : thread_state) (cs : core_state),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    update_thread_state 0 th' cs = { thread_states := [(0, none, th')], io := cs.io }
----
def CerberusHeapLang.valImg :
Int → List CerbMem.AbsByte
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromMemValue_int :
∀ (ety : integerType) (iv : CerbMem.IntegerValue),
  (valueFromMemValue (CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety iv)).snd = Vloaded (LVspecified (OVinteger iv))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromMemValue_ptr :
∀ (t : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  (valueFromMemValue (CerbMem.MemValue.MVpointer t pv)).snd = Vloaded (LVspecified (OVpointer pv))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromPexpr_val :
∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), valueFromPexpr (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromPexprs_pair :
∀ (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] =
    match valueFromPexpr pe1, valueFromPexpr pe2 with
    | some v1, some v2 => some [v1, v2]
    | x, x_1 => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (ds : List dyn_annotation) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
      E {{ v, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds v) }} ⊢
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e), ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
      E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a : List annot) (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Φ₁ Φ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
      Φ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsA v) = Φ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsB v)) →
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c), ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
        E {{ Φ₁ }} ⊢
      WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c), ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
        E {{ Φ₂ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_env_invariant_stable :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e →
    ∀ {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
      WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢
        WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ; E {{ v, ⌜v.ρ = ρ⌝ ∗ Φ v }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (mo : memory_order)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊢
      WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo, ρ := ρ, M := M } @ s ;
        E {{
          w,
            ∃ fp,
              ⌜w =
                    {
                      w :=
                        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp]
                          (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs),
                      ρ := ρ, M := M }⌝ ∗
                pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal)
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  Φ v ⊢ WP (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt v) @ s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_sseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a pa : List annot) (bty : core_base_type)
  (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e1, ρ := ev0 :: evs, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
      E {{
        v,
          ▷
            WP { e := e2, ρ := v.ρ, M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @ s ;
              E {{ w, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.mergeInto v w) }} }} ⊢
    WP
      { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2), ρ := ev0 :: evs,
        M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @
      s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (cv : value)
  (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ⊢
        WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo, ρ := ρ, M := M } @ s ;
          E {{
            w,
              ∃ fp,
                ⌜w =
                      { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit, ρ := ρ,
                        M := M }⌝ ∗
                  pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          ((CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre.contractive :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF),
  Iris.OFE.Contractive (CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre M Ls)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (ds : List dyn_annotation)
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun w ρ' => Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w) ρ') e ρ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (a : List annot)
  (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      Ψ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsA w) ρ' =
        Ψ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsB w) ρ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ₁ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c)) ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ₂ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_arr_elem_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M' : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls' : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann' : core_run_annotation) (aty' : ctype) (id' a' : Int) (i : Nat)
  (mo' : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  4 * i + CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty' →
    (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a' + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
            (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs')) =
          mv) →
      CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV CerberusHeapLang.intTy mv = false →
        (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id' dq { addr := a', ty := aty', bytes := bs' } ∗
            ∀ fp,
              CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id' dq { addr := a', ty := aty', bytes := bs' } -∗
                Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps M' Ls' Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id' (a' + ↑(4 * i))) mo')
            ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_case_value :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)) {cval : value}
  {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  valueFromPexpr pe = some cval →
    select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e' ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_create :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (aprov : CerbMem.Provenance) (alignN : Int)
  (ty : ctype) (pref : prefix0) (la nid : Int) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  0 < CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty →
    CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy ty = false →
      CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) ≠ 0 →
        CerberusHeapLang.decIndep (CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty)) ty
            (List.replicate (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) CerberusHeapLang.undefByte) →
          CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn { lastAddr := la, nextId := nid } ∗
              (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr nid
                      (CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN
                        (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty)) ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1}
                    ty ; List.replicate (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) CerberusHeapLang.undefByte ∗
                  CerberusHeapLang.cursorOwn
                    { lastAddr := CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty),
                      nextId := nid + 1 } -∗
                Ψ
                  (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure
                    (Vobject
                      (OVpointer
                        (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr nid
                          (CerberusHeapLang.freshBase la alignN (CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty))))))
                  ρ) ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
              (CerberusHeapLang.createExpr loc ann (CerbMem.IntegerValue.IV aprov alignN) ty pref) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_seq_stores :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation)
  (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
      y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes))
      (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
      (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_store_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs' ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs'))
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} {R : Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e ρ ∗ R ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun w ρ' => iprop(Ψ w ρ' ∗ R)) e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_if_false :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e3 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ
----

  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}

    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e2 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    (pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ∗
        ∀ fp,
          pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs -∗
            Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs))
              ρ) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (mo : memory_order) (dqm dqb : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) vty bs = mv) →
    CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV vty mv = false →
      (CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm dqb vty bs ∗
          ∀ fp,
            CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm dqb vty bs -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
          (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_cell_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) vty
            (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty) (List.drop off bs)) =
          mv) →
      CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV vty mv = false →
        (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ∗
            ∀ fp,
              CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } -∗
                Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
  (mo : memory_order) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_node_field :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (off : Nat) (mo : memory_order)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  off + 8 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy →
    (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
            (List.take 8 (List.drop off bs)) =
          mv) →
      (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
          ∀ fp,
            CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq
                { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
          (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
            (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo)
          ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_memop_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (mop : memop)
  (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) {v1 v2 : value} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 = some v1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some v2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1), Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)]) ρ ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [pe1, pe2]) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_memop_ptreq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue) {b : Bool} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  (∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ = some (b, σ)) →
    Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)) ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
        (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)) (Vobject (OVpointer pv2))) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  Ψ w ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ
