theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_pure :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {v : value},
  valueFromPexpr pe = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v →
      Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure pe)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_run :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
  (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern (ev0 :: evs) pes = some vs →
      Ls l vs (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Erun ra l pes)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_save :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (sb : sym × core_base_type)
  (ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) {cvals : List value} (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) = some cvals →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ body (CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs)) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Esave sb ps body)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun w ρ' => CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2 ρ') e1
      (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq_spec :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa pb : List annot)
  (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun w ρ' =>
        iprop(∃ ov,
            ⌜w.val = Vloaded (LVspecified ov)⌝ ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2
                (update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) ρ')))
      e1 (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq_sym :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun w ρ' =>
        iprop(∃ v,
            ⌜w = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v⌝ ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e2 (update_env (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) v ρ')))
      e1 (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_sound :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs M Ls Ψ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e ρ -∗
      WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ; ⊤ {{ w, Ψ w.w w.ρ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      (pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ∗
          ∀ fp,
            pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (dqm : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ vty)) cv = some mv →
    CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible vty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
      (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty →
            (CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm (Iris.DFrac.own 1) vty bs ∗
                ∀ fp,
                  CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm (Iris.DFrac.own 1) vty
                      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd -∗
                    Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
              CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
                (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) cv
                  mo)
                ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store_cell_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ vty)) cv = some mv →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
      CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible vty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
          (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
              (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty →
              CerberusHeapLang.decIndep a aty
                  (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd bs) →
                (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                      { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ∗
                    ∀ fp,
                      CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                          { addr := a, ty := aty,
                            bytes :=
                              CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd
                                bs } -∗
                        Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
                  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
                    (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off))
                      cv mo)
                    ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
  (pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (mo : memory_order) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value},
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    valueFromPexpr pe3 = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 = some cv →
          CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo) ρ ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store_node_field :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (off : Nat) (cv : value)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy)) cv = some mv →
    off + 8 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy →
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = 8 →
        CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
          (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
            (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
              (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                    { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
                  ∀ fp,
                    CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                        { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy,
                          bytes :=
                            CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd
                              bs } -∗
                      Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
                CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
                  (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) cv mo)
                  ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_struct_x_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order) (id a : Int)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
        { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy, bytes := bs } ∗
      ∀ fp,
        CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
            { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.structTy,
              bytes :=
                CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes CerberusHeapLang.fieldX CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes
                  bs } -∗
          Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy
        (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑CerberusHeapLang.fieldX)) CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
      ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_struct_y_store :

  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}

      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy
        (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑CerberusHeapLang.fieldY)) CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo)
      ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_unfold :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ e ρ ⊣⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre M Ls (CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls) Ψ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_value_inv :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ ⊢ |={⊤}=> Ψ w ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ₁ e ρ ⊢
    (∀ w ρ', Ψ₁ w ρ' -∗ Ψ₂ w ρ') -∗ CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ₂ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_wseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls
      (fun w ρ' => CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2 ρ') e1
      (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Ewseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wpt :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF →
          Nat →
            (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt.eq_def :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF) (x : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls x =
    match (motive :=
      Nat →
        (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
          CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF)
      x with
    | 0 => CerberusHeapLang.wpt.pre M Ls 0 fun x x_1 x_2 => iprop(False)
    | k.succ => CerberusHeapLang.wpt.pre M Ls (k + 1) (CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wpt.pre :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF →
          Nat →
            ((CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
                CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
                CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF} (ds : List dyn_annotation)
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {k : Nat}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k (fun w ρ' => Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w) ρ') e ρ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF} (a : List annot)
  (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {k : Nat} {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      Ψ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsA w) ρ' =
        Ψ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsB w) ρ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ₁ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c)) ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ₂ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_det_step :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {k : Nat},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none →
      (∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem), CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ)) →
        (∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
            (out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
            CerberusHeapLang.Step M (e, ρ, σ) out → out = (e', ρ', σ)) →
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e' ρ' ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_drive_aux :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.SeqWF →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
          CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot cont ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
        ∀ (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF) (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) (k : Nat)
          (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
          (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns nt : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag e →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              Iris.stateInterp σ ns [] nt ∗
                  CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT M Ls (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) ∗
                    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) e (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
                |={⊤|}=> ⌜CerberusHeapLang.DriveDoneAt M aids k e (ev0 :: evs) σ ψ⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_engine_boundJ :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap}
  {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
          CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot cont ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
        ∀ (Ls : [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF] → CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF)
          (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev00 : Fmap sym value) (evs0 : List (Fmap sym value))
          (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag e₀ →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot e₀ ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
                ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) (k : Nat),
                  (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
                      ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
                        CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) Ls
                            (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) ∗
                          CerberusHeapLang.wpt (CerberusHeapLang.procCtx p rs) Ls k
                            (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) →
                    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                      ∃ v σ',
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids k
                              (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ =
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' ∧
                          ψ v σ' ∧
                            ((CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e₀ = true ∧
                                ∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type))
                                  (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                                  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
                                    CerberusHeapLang.stateInert cont = true) →
                              σ' = σ₀)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_engine_boundU :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx},
  M.SeqWF →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag cont) →
      (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
          CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot cont ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
        ∀ (Ls : [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF] → CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF)
          (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev00 : Fmap sym value) (evs0 : List (Fmap sym value))
          (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
          CerberusHeapLang.Frag e₀ →
            CerberusHeapLang.pot e₀ ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
                ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) (k : Nat),
                  (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
                      ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
                        CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT M Ls (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) ∗
                          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k (CerberusHeapLang.readoutPost ψ) e₀
                            (ev00 :: evs0)) →
                    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                      ∃ v σ',
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveU M aids k (M.thread e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ =
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' ∧
                          ψ v σ' ∧
                            (CerberusHeapLang.stateInert e₀ = true ∧
                                CerberusHeapLang.StateInertLabels M →
                              σ' = σ₀)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_if_false :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {k : Nat},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vfalse →
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e3 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_if_true :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {k : Nat},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ g = some Vtrue →
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e2 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_jump_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (k : Nat)
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) →
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e ρ =
        iprop(|={⊤}=>
            ∃ params cont vs ev0 evs m,
              ⌜ρ = ev0 :: evs⌝ ∗
                ⌜CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont)⌝ ∗
                  ⌜CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern ρ pes = some vs⌝ ∗
                    ⌜1 + m ≤ k⌝ ∗ Ls l m vs ρ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_jump_frame_sseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (pat : pattern) {e1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)} {k k' : Nat},
  k ≤ k' →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal e1 = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1 = some (l, pes) →
        CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ₁ e1 ρ ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k' Ψ₂ (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_load_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (mo : memory_order) (dqm dqb : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) vty bs = mv) →
      CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV vty mv = false →
        (CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm dqb vty bs ∗
            ∀ fp,
              CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm dqb vty bs -∗
                Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_load_cell_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
      (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) vty
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty) (List.drop off bs)) =
            mv) →
        CerberusHeapLang.loadTrapV vty mv = false →
          (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ∗
              ∀ fp,
                CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } -∗
                  Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
              (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_load_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
  (mo : memory_order) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : Nat},
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_load_node_field :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (off : Nat) (mo : memory_order)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    off + 8 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy →
      (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
              (List.take 8 (List.drop off bs)) =
            mv) →
        (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
            ∀ fp,
              CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id dq
                  { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } -∗
                Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
              (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo)
            ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_memop_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (mop : memop)
  (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) {v1 v2 : value} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {k : Nat},
  valueFromPexprs [pe1, pe2] = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe1 = some v1 →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some v2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
            (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [Pexpr [] () (PEval v1), Pexpr [] () (PEval v2)]) ρ ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.memopRedex mop [pe1, pe2]) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_memop_ptreq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (pv1 pv2 : CerbMem.PointerValue) {b : Bool} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {k : Nat},
  2 ≤ k →
    (∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.eqPtrval default pv1 pv2) σ = some (b, σ)) →
      Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue b)) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
          (CerberusHeapLang.memopPtrEqVals (Vobject (OVpointer pv1)) (Vobject (OVpointer pv2))) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_mono :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack), Ψ₁ w ρ' ⊢ Ψ₂ w ρ') →
    ∀ (k : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ₁ e ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ₂ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_mono_k :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} {k k' : Nat},
  k ≤ k' →
    ∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k' Ψ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {k : Nat},
  CerberusHeapLang.deliveryCost w ≤ k →
    Ψ w ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_pure :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {v : value} {k : Nat},
  2 ≤ k →
    valueFromPexpr pe = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe = some v →
        Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ρ ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure pe)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_run :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
  (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (m : Nat) {k : Nat},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel M.labels l = some (params, cont) →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs M.extern (ev0 :: evs) pes = some vs →
      1 + m ≤ k →
        Ls l m vs (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Erun ra l pes)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_save :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (sb : sym × core_base_type)
  (ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) {cvals : List value} (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) {k : Nat},
  valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) = some cvals →
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ body (CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs)) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Esave sb ps body)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (k1 k2 : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k1
      (fun w ρ' => CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k2 (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2 ρ') e1
      (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k1 + k2) Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_seq_spec :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa pb : List annot)
  (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (k1 k2 : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k1
      (fun w ρ' =>
        iprop(∃ ov,
            ⌜w.val = Vloaded (LVspecified ov)⌝ ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k2 (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2
                (update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) ρ')))
      e1 (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k1 + k2) Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_seq_sym :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (k1 k2 : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k1
      (fun w ρ' =>
        iprop(∃ v,
            ⌜w = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v⌝ ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k2 Ψ e2
                (update_env (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) v ρ')))
      e1 (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k1 + k2) Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.symPat pa x bty) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_sound :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (k : Nat)
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT M Ls Ψ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e ρ -∗
      WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ; ⊤ [{ w, Ψ w.w w.ρ }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_step_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (k : Nat)
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ e ρ =
        iprop(∀ σ₁ ns obs nt,
            Iris.stateInterp σ₁ ns obs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
              ⌜Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.Reducible ({ e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M }, σ₁)⌝ ∗
                ∀ r σ₂ eₜ,
                  ⌜Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep ({ e := e, ρ := ρ, M := M }, σ₁) []
                        (r, σ₂, eₜ)⌝ ={∅,⊤}=∗
                    Iris.stateInterp σ₂ (ns + 1) obs nt ∗ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ r.e r.ρ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_store_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (dqm : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ vty)) cv = some mv →
      CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible vty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
          (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
              (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty →
              (CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm (Iris.DFrac.own 1) vty bs ∗
                  ∀ fp,
                    CerberusHeapLang.pointsToView id a aty off dqm (Iris.DFrac.own 1) vty
                        (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd -∗
                      Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
                CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
                  (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann vty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off))
                    cv mo)
                  ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_store_cell_at :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype) (off : Nat)
  (vty : ctype) (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ vty)) cv = some mv →
      off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
        CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible vty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
          (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
            (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
              (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype vty →
                CerberusHeapLang.decIndep a aty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd bs) →
                  (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                        { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ∗
                      ∀ fp,
                        CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                            { addr := a, ty := aty,
                              bytes :=
                                CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd
                                  bs } -∗
                          Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
                    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
                      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann vty
                        (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) cv mo)
                      ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_store_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype)
  (pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (mo : memory_order) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {k : Nat},
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    valueFromPexpr pe3 = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr M.extern ρ pe3 = some cv →
          CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo) ρ ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls (k + 1) Ψ
              (CerberusHeapLang.storeOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 pe3 mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_store_node_field :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (id a : Int) (off : Nat) (cv : value)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat},
  3 ≤ k →
    memValueFromValue M.tagDefs (Ctype [] (unatomic_ CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy)) cv = some mv →
      off + 8 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy →
        (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd.length = 8 →
          CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
            (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
              (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                (CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                      { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy, bytes := bs } ∗
                    ∀ fp,
                      CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn id (Iris.DFrac.own 1)
                          { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.nodeTy,
                            bytes :=
                              CerberusHeapLang.spliceBytes off (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd
                                bs } -∗
                        Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
                  CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ
                    (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.nodePtrTy
                      (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) cv mo)
                    ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_strongly_normalizing :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx}
  (Ls : [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF] → CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF)
  (Ψ :
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF)
  (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
          ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, CerberusHeapLang.cellOwn i (Iris.DFrac.own 1) c) ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecsT M Ls Ψ ∗ CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e₀ ρ₀) →
        Relation.StronglyNormalizing Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep
          ([{ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, M := M }], σ₀)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_val_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (k : Nat)
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = some w →
    CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls k Ψ e ρ = iprop(⌜CerberusHeapLang.deliveryCost w ≤ k⌝ ∗ |={⊤}=> Ψ w ρ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wpt_zero_step_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpecT GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none → CerberusHeapLang.wpt M Ls 0 Ψ e ρ = iprop(False)
----

def CerberusHeapLang.wseqLs :

∀ (v1 v2 : value), CerberusHeapLang.Frag (CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (M : CerberusHeapLang.MachineCtx) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF) (v1 v2 : value)
  (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps M Ls (fun w x => iprop(⌜w.val = v2⌝)) (CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2)
      (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wseq_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (v2 : value),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx CerberusHeapLang.wseqLs fun w x =>
      iprop(⌜w.val = v2⌝)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wseq_certified :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (v1 v2 : value)
  (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  2 + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
            (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2)) σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2))
            σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v' : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
                (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread (CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2)) σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v' σ' →
            v' = v2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wseq_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (v1 v2 : value),
  ⊢
    WP
      { e := CerberusHeapLang.wseqProg v1 v2, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv,
        M := CerberusHeapLang.spikeCtx } @
      Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
      ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜w.val = v2⌝ }}
----

theorem apply_ctx.eq_def :
∀ (ctx : context) (expr1 : generic_expr core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  apply_ctx ctx expr1 =
    match ctx with
    | CTX => expr1
    | Cunseq annot1 es1 ctx' es2 =>
      generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eunseq (es1 ++ apply_ctx ctx' expr1 :: es2))
    | Cwseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ewseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Csseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Esseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Cannot annot1 xs ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eannot xs (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
    | Cbound annot1 ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ebound (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
----
theorem assoc_adjust.eq_def :
∀ {a b : Type} [inst : Lem_Basic_classes.Eq0 a] (f : b → b) (k : a) (x : List (a × b)),
  assoc_adjust f k x =
    match x with
    | [] => []
    | (k', v) :: xs => if (k == k') = true then (k, f v) :: xs else (k', v) :: assoc_adjust f k xs
----
theorem drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
  drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc =
    match (motive :=
      Nat →
        List Nat →
          ndM (Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) step_kind driver_error
            (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st)
    | lemFuel.succ => fun x =>
      match x with
      | [] => nd_return acc
      | tid1 :: xs' =>
        nd_bind
          (nd_read fun dr_st =>
            have th_info :=
              match Lem_List.lookupBy (fun x y => x == y) tid1 dr_st.core_state0.thread_states with
              | some z => z
              | none =>
                failwithI
                  ("Driver.drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2 => invalid tid: ".append
                    (Lem_String_extra.stringFromNat tid1));
            step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs dr_st.layout_state dr_st.core_file dr_st.core_extern tid1
              th_info)
          fun steps =>
          match find_can_advance steps with
          | none =>
            drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs
              (fmapAddBy defaultCompare tid1 steps acc) xs'
          | some step1 =>
            nd_bind (advance_step _lemReader_tagDefs tid1 step1) fun wakeups =>
              have xs'' :=
                match wakeups with
                | NOWAKEUP => tid1 :: xs'
                | WAKEUP false tids => tid1 :: list_inserts tids xs'
                | WAKEUP true tids => list_inserts tids xs';
              drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc xs''
----
theorem driver2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (with_concurrency : Bool),
  driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      nd_bind (new_drive_core_threads _lemReader_tagDefs ()) fun tid_steps =>
        nd_bind nd_get fun post_core_dr_st =>
          have _non_blocked_th_sts :=
            List.filter
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (tid1, th_info) =>
                  (step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs post_core_dr_st.layout_state
                        post_core_dr_st.core_file post_core_dr_st.core_extern tid1 th_info).any
                    fun step1 => step1 != Step_blocked2)
              post_core_dr_st.core_state0.thread_states;
          if
              Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) (CerbGlobal.current_execution_mode ())
                  (some CerbGlobal.ExecutionMode.random) =
                true then
            bindExhaustive (pick (SK_misc ["driver 2"]) tid_steps) fun p =>
              match p with
              | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                match step_opt with
                | some step1 =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
                | none => nd_return ()
          else
            have non_blocked :=
              List.filter
                (fun p =>
                  match p with
                  | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                    !Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) step_opt (some Step_blocked2))
                tid_steps;
            nd_bind (if (non_blocked.length == 0) = true then nd_return () else nd_return ())
              fun u =>
              nd_bind (pick (SK_misc ["driver non_blocked"]) non_blocked) fun x =>
                match x with
                | (fst, none) => nd_return ()
                | (fst, some step1) =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
----
theorem eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (loc1 : CerbLocation.Loc) (current_call_loc_opt : Option CerbLocation.Loc)
  (core_extern1 : Fmap sym sym) (env1 : List (Fmap sym value))
  (mem_st_opt : Option CerbMem.MemState) (file1 : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs loc1 current_call_loc_opt core_extern1 env1
      mem_st_opt file1 pe =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (Result (Undef CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match
        have pulled := pull_constrained 0 pe;
        pulled with
      | pulled_pe@h:(Pexpr a PUnit.unit (PEconstrained a_1)) =>
        exception_undef_return (Sum.inl pulled_pe)
      | pulled_pe =>
        exception_undef_bind
          (step_eval_pexpr _lemReader_tagDefs 0 loc1 current_call_loc_opt core_extern1 env1
            mem_st_opt file1 false pulled_pe)
          fun pe' =>
          match valueFromPexpr pe' with
          | some cval => exception_undef_return (Sum.inr cval)
          | none =>
            eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs loc1 current_call_loc_opt
              core_extern1 env1 mem_st_opt file1 pe'
----
theorem get_loc.eq_def :
∀ (annots1 : List annot),
  get_loc annots1 =
    match annots1 with
    | [] => none
    | Aloc loc1 :: tail => some loc1
    | Astd a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Auid a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker_object_types a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Abmc a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aattrs a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Atypedef a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Alabel a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Acerb a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Avalue a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Ainlined_label a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Astmt :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aexpr :: annots' => get_loc annots'
----
theorem is_unseq_with_ccall_aux.eq_def :
∀ (acc : Bool) (x : context),
  is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc x =
    match x with
    | CTX => acc
    | Cunseq a es1 ctx' es2 =>
      have acc' := acc || (es1 ++ es2).any has_ccall;
      is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc' ctx'
    | Cwseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Csseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cannot a a_1 ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cbound a ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux false ctx'
----
theorem liftAction_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2),
  liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr =
    match (motive := Nat → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown [])
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match (motive :=
        (st2 → st1) →
          (st2 → st1 → st2) →
            (info1 → info2) →
              (err1 → err2) → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
        get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr => fun x =>
        match x with
        | NDactive a1 => NDactive a1
        | NDkilled r =>
          NDkilled
            (match r with
            | Undef0 loc1 ubs => Undef0 loc1 ubs
            | Error0 loc1 str => Error0 loc1 str
            | Other err => Other (liftErr err))
        | NDnd info str_ms =>
          NDnd (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m_act) =>
                  (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act))
              str_ms)
        | NDguard info cs1 act =>
          NDguard (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act)
        | NDbranch info cs1 m_act1 m_act2 =>
          NDbranch (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act1)
            (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act2)
        | NDstep info info_ms =>
          NDstep (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m) => (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m))
              info_ms)
----
theorem liftND_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2)
  (n : ndM a info1 err1 cs st1),
  liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr n =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, n with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, ND m =>
        ND fun st21 =>
          match m (get2 st21) with
          | (act, st1') =>
            (liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act, put1 st21 st1')
----
theorem lookup_env.eq_def :
∀ {a b : Type} [inst : Lem_Map.MapKeyType b] (sym1 : b) (x : List (Fmap b a)),
  lookup_env sym1 x =
    match x with
    | [] => none
    | env1 :: xs =>
      match fmapLookupBy Lem_Map.mapKeyCompare sym1 env1 with
      | none => lookup_env sym1 xs
      | some ret => some ret
----
theorem nd_bind_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a b c d e f : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (n : ndM f b d a c) (f1 : f → ndM e b d a c),
  nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel n f1 =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match n, f1 with
      | ND m_act, f1 =>
        ND fun st =>
          match m_act st with
          | (NDactive z, st') =>
            match f1 z with
            | ND m_act' => m_act' st'
          | (NDkilled r, st') => (NDkilled r, st')
          | (NDnd debug_str str_ms, st') =>
            (NDnd debug_str
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
          | (NDguard debug_str cs m_act', st') =>
            (NDguard debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act' f1), st')
          | (NDbranch debug_str cs m_act1 m_act2, st') =>
            (NDbranch debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act1 f1)
                (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act2 f1),
              st')
          | (NDstep info str_ms, st') =>
            (NDstep info
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
----
theorem update_env_aux_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a : Type} [inst : Lem_Map.MapKeyType a] (lemFuel : Nat) (g : generic_pattern a) (cval : value)
  (env1 : Fmap a value),
  update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel g cval env1 =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted env1
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match g, cval, env1 with
      | Pattern a_1 pat, cval, env1 =>
        match pat, cval with
        | CaseBase (none, snd), x => env1
        | CaseBase (some sym1, snd), x => fmapAddBy Lem_Map.mapKeyCompare sym1 cval env1
        | CaseCtor (Cnil a) [], Vlist a_2 [] => env1
        | CaseCtor Ccons [pat1, pat2], Vlist bTy_elem (cval1 :: cvals) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat1 cval1
            (update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat2 (Vlist bTy_elem cvals) env1)
        | CaseCtor Ctuple pats', Vtuple cvals =>
          List.foldr
            (fun p acc =>
              match p, acc with
              | (pat', cval'), acc => update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' cval' acc)
            env1 (pats'.zip cvals)
        | CaseCtor Cspecified [pat'], Vloaded (LVspecified oval) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' (Vobject oval) env1
        | CaseCtor Cunspecified [pat'], Vloaded (LVunspecified ty1) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' (Vctype ty1) env1
        | CaseCtor ctor1 pats, x =>
          have str_ctor :=
            match ctor1 with
            | Cnil a => "nil"
            | Ccons => "cons"
            | Ctuple => "tuple"
            | Carray => "array"
            | Civmax => "ivmax"
            | Civmin => "ivmin"
            | Civsizeof => "ivsizeof"
            | Civalignof => "ivalignof"
            | CivCOMPL => "ivCOMPL"
            | CivAND => "ivAND"
            | CivOR => "ivOR"
            | CivXOR => "ivXOR"
            | Cspecified => "specified"
            | Cunspecified => "unspecified"
            | Cfvfromint => "fvfromint"
            | Civfromfloat => "ivfromfloat"
            | CivNULLcap is_signed =>
              "ivNULLcap(".append (if is_signed = true then "signed" else "unsigned".append ")");
          failwithI
            ("WIP: Core_aux.update_env_aux ==> ctor= ".append
              (str_ctor.append
                (", |pats|= ".append
                  ((Lem_String_extra.stringFromNat pats.length).append
                    (" -- ".append (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cval))))))
----
